## Summary

Snapback 4 is one of two parallel candidates for the successor to Snapback 2,
and the sibling of Snapback 3, not its successor. It builds
[LLP 2000.000](./2000.000-keep-the-core-flip-two-substrates.rfc.md) as
written: the Snapback 2 core kept verbatim, two substrates flipped up front —
resources metered at runtime instead of priced statically, and a client that
replicates the viewer's declared partition instead of caching receipted
results — proved first on the hub fixture, with a deterministic simulator as
the primary harness. Snapback 3 ([LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md), led by
Codex / GPT-6 Astra) keeps the same two questions open and discovers their
answers in its first slice. Snapback 2 ([LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md))
continues as the incumbent and the control. All three are judged by one
pre-registered comparison on one clock.

This document records the author's decisions of 2026-09-12 that created the
track, restates the bet on one page so a reader can orient without the brief,
fixes the bakeoff protocol, draws the fence, and plans the first four weeks.
The design argument and its evidence stay in LLP 2000.000. Only running code
will bind (`rules/RULES.md`).

## 1. Decisions (author, 2026-09-12)

On 2026-09-12 Charlie Cheever asked whether Claude Fable 5.1 should lead a
Snapback 4 while Codex / GPT-6 Astra leads Snapback 3 and Snapback 2
continues, "and see which one turns out best." The lead answered yes with
conditions; the author accepted them ("Sounds good") and asked for this
document. The decisions below are therefore the lead's proposal as accepted
by the author. Where the author has not separately ruled, the text says so.

1. **Three tracks, one comparison.** Snapback 2 continues as the incumbent
   and the control. Snapback 3 builds LLP 2000; Snapback 4 builds LLP
   2000.000. This is a bakeoff of *bets* — discover the substrates in the
   slice versus decide them first — not of models. The result will say more
   about the bets than about the leads, and §3 is written so that it can.
2. **The name is Snapback 4.** Siblings, not a sequence: "4" does not mean
   "after 3." A separate number gives the track its own corpus root,
   directory, package identity and data directory with no rename when the
   bakeoff resolves, and files never move in LLP. If Snapback 4 loses, it is
   a number that never shipped. "Snapback 3b" was considered and rejected:
   it reads as a variant of 3, and this is a different bet.
3. **Snapback 4 builds LLP 2000.000 as written.** Its §14 open questions are
   the lead's to decide inside the track, recorded in §7 as they are
   decided. The author rules on the bar, the comparison and promotion (§3).
4. **One owner on the core.** The lead writes the engine, the store and the
   client contract. Codex lanes build the fixture generator, the reference
   applications, the device witnesses and the periphery. Blind reviews from
   two model families are reserved for the two disclosure-sensitive
   mechanisms: partition inversion of read rules (LLP 2000.000 §7.1) and
   the meter's cut behaviour on non-uniform scans (LLP 2000.000 §6). This
   departs from the standing rule that Codex codes and Claude orchestrates,
   deliberately, for the core only.
5. **The clock is four weeks** from the first commit under `snapback4/` to
   the first slice running on the hub fixture. At the clock the author kills
   the track, merges its substrates into a survivor, or continues it. There
   is no third month without a ruling.
6. **Shared and not shared.** Shared with Snapback 3: the LLP corpus, the
   Clique harness and scorers, the fixtures, the briefs, and the simulator
   once either track has one. Not shared: code, except the Snapback 2 pieces
   both are expected to copy (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).
7. **Snapback 2 keeps shipping and freezes new substrate work for the
   duration.** Fix-forward, the loop bar, acquisition and papercuts continue;
   no new certificate axes, client custody mechanisms or ceiling changes.
   This is accepted here as a condition of the bakeoff. Making it binding on
   concurrent Snapback 2 sessions needs one line in `rules/NOT-DOING.md`'s
   Moves ledger, which this document does not write.
8. **Reading load.** This root, updated in place, and one append-only
   journal, `snapback4/JOURNAL.md`. Sub-LLPs only for a decision that must
   outlive the journal. No panels beyond decision 4's two reviews.

## 2. The bet on one page

**Keep verbatim from Snapback 2** (LLP 2000.000 §5.1): the rule language
with its uniformity and hoist analysis, `decided_true` and the
structural-fact refusals; one data language, Quarry, with `--` comments,
`require … else CODE`, `shape`, `merge`, `walk`, `chunk`; the value model
and its conformance corpus; typed read and write states with no silent state
and the refusal envelope `{code, family, site, message, rewrite, guide}`; the
successor classifier (widening additive, checked narrowing, exact projection
ownership); `deliver`'s engine-computed audience, tombstones and
`bootstrap`; `schedule`, `job`, `chunk` and rehearsal; personas; `init`,
`dev`, `check`, `doctor`, `run`, `test`, `why`, `trace`, `guide`; H0
`deploy`, `rollback`, `secret`; channels; assets; the word-prefix index.

**Flip 1 — certify authorization, meter resources** (LLP 2000.000 §6). The
certificate keeps every read site's shape, uniformity, completeness class
and prediction eligibility, and stops pricing bytes and steps. A
deterministic meter charges examined rows, index entries, rule probes,
steps, heap, output and rows written as the program runs, on the server and
in the client interpreter alike. A query cut by the meter returns `capped`
with no continuation past the cut, the same observable outcome as a static
ceiling cut today; a mutation aborts with `E_BOUND` naming actual work; a
job is chunked. `check --cost` prints the structural cost class; `test`
prints measured meters on the hub fixture.

**Flip 2 — the viewer's partition is the unit of sync** (LLP 2000.000 §7).
`sync to <audience> last N by <index>` on a table makes the engine maintain,
per principal, the rows that principal may read, as a change log with a
horizon; a table without the word is online-only, and `check` says which
rule term prevented inversion. The client holds a replica of its partitions,
one watermark each, a durable outbox with client-minted ids, and the
interpreter; queries run locally and are `complete` inside the horizon.
Revocation is a scope tombstone; `until-revoked` and `delivered-history`
survive as the client's leave policy. Two client tiers — online-only
without a store, local-first with one — share one language and one server.

**The vocabulary is fixed before the mechanism** (LLP 2000.000 §8):

```ts
type Read<T> = { data: T; complete: boolean; fresh: boolean; since?: number; next?: Cursor }
            | { loading: true } | { denied: Refusal };
row.pending: boolean;
type Write = { state: "pending" } | { state: "sent"; seq: number } | { state: "failed"; why: Refusal };
type Link = "online" | "offline" | "signed-out";
```

A screen that needs a word not on that card is a defect in the mechanism,
not a gap in the card.

**The hub comes first** (LLP 2000.000 §11): the Clique generator's `u0001`
with 1,000 memberships, `u0500` with 500 follows, and about 100,000
messages are the first fixture; day-two changes are designed before the
day-one screen; the native client is in slice one; physical floors are
measured before targets; the simulator is the primary harness.

## 3. The bakeoff protocol

Shared with Snapback 3; LLP 2000 or a sibling record should adopt the same
text so that the two tracks are measured by one instrument.

**Brief.** [`benchmarks/briefs/social-messenger.md`](../benchmarks/briefs/social-messenger.md)
(Clique) M1–M8, plus
[`benchmarks/briefs/messaging-day2-evolution.md`](../benchmarks/briefs/messaging-day2-evolution.md).
The field-work application (LLP 2000 §4) tests generality after the first
slice, on whichever tracks survive the clock.

**Fixture.** The Clique generator at the instrument freeze LLP 1023 and
LLP 1024 record (`f40559f37722c79178be664a11f8a6485f1508fd`): hub `u0001`
with 100 groups and 900 DMs, `u0500` with 500 follows, and the 100,015
messages of the retained 0.0.25 replay database. The same fixture for every
track and for the control.

**Harness and judge.** [`benchmarks/protocol.md`](../benchmarks/protocol.md);
the same three scorers as the 2026-09-05 run (gpt-6-astra, grok-4.6,
muse-spark); Convex as the control arm; published packages; fresh cold
agents with only the installed package and the task. Neither lead runs the
comparison or grades their own track. The author rules on the numbers.

**Decision rule, pre-registered.**
# LLP 2000.000: Snapback 3 — keep the core, flip two substrates, start at hub scale

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Draft
**Role:** Second opinion on LLP 2000; since 2026-09-12 also the design brief Snapback 4 builds (LLP 3000)
**Systems:** Snapback 3 product architecture, bounded computation, client data model, client API vocabulary, language and tooling, effects runtime, verification (simulation), performance budgets, process
**Author:** Claude Fable 5.1, at Charlie Cheever's request
**Date:** 2026-09-12
**Revised:** 2026-09-12 (adopted as the Snapback 4 design brief by LLP 3000 decision 3); 2026-09-12 (initial)
**Related:** [LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md) (parent), [LLP 3000](./3000-snapback4.explainer.md) (the Snapback 4 root that builds this brief), LLP 0000, [LLP 0329](./0329-agent-native-rebuild-diagnosis.research.md), [LLP 0329.014.004](./0329.014.004-bounded-data-programs-v1-design.rfc.md), [LLP 0329.016](./0329.016-agentic-dev-loop-from-scratch.rfc.md), [LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md), [LLP 1001](./1001-query-engine.spec.md), [LLP 1002](./1002-dev-loop.spec.md), [LLP 1012](./1012-local-first-v1.spec.md), [LLP 1018.003](./1018.003-truthful-and-composed-client.decision.md), [LLP 1018.008](./1018.008-elegance-after-the-corrections.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.007](./1018.008.007-affordable-offline-acquisition.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.008](./1018.008.008-locality-of-application-changes.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.009](./1018.008.009-residual-ergonomic-gaps.research.md), [LLP 1019](./1019-derived-delivery-custody.rfc.md), [LLP 1020](./1020-day-two-evolution-and-legible-ceilings.rfc.md), [LLP 1023](./1023-clique-builder-jump.rfc.md), [LLP 1024](./1024-single-lane-delivered-search.rfc.md), [LLP 1025](./1025-digest-final-emission-at-hub-scale.research.md), `rules/RULES.md`, `rules/NOT-DOING.md`, `benchmarks/runs/2026-09-05-clique-full-3x3/holistic-scores.md`

## 1. Summary

LLP 2000 is right that the whole application is the unit of design, right
that ownership comes before abstraction, and right to ask for deterministic
simulation and an early real deployment. Its weakness is that it treats the
two structural choices that produced Snapback 2's costs as open research
questions, and then proposes a first experiment at exactly the scale where
those costs are invisible. This document takes a position on both, names
what to keep from Snapback 2 verbatim, and rewrites the first slice so it
can fail for the right reasons.

The position, in one paragraph. Snapback 2's *core* is excellent and should
be copied: the rule language and its uniformity analysis, the structural-fact
family, one data language, typed states with no silence, refusals that paste
a rewrite, personas, the successor classifier, the derived per-recipient
log, the loop verbs and their sub-second budgets. Two *substrates* under
that core are where the record shows the hours went, and both should be
flipped rather than re-investigated: (1) the static certificate should keep
certifying **authorization and shape** and stop pricing **bytes and steps**,
which move to a deterministic runtime meter with honest `capped`/abort
outcomes; and (2) the client should hold **a replica of the viewer's declared
partition** — the local-first shape every shipped sync engine and Snapback
2's own `deliver` log converge on — instead of a per-query result cache
proven by receipts. Those two flips dissolve most of the machinery that
leaked into the application (receipts, coverage, halos, validators, grants,
ID slices, drains, `withHistory`, follower inboxes, per-table retention
words) and turn the 28-second acquisition into a bulk stream whose floor is
the device's write speed. The first slice should be built on the **hub
fixture** (1,000 memberships, 500 follows, 100,000 messages), with the
day-two change designed before the day-one screen, a native client in the
first slice, and a deterministic simulator as the primary test harness.

Three claims this document makes that LLP 2000 does not:

1. **The costs have two named causes, and both are decisions, not
   mysteries.** Byte-priced static certification and the receipt-proven
   result cache account for every ergonomic residual in LLP 1018.008.009
   except documentation. §2 shows the mapping.
2. **The first experiment must include the hub.** Snapback 2 was "elegant on
   day one and hostile on day two" (LLP 1020) and broke at 1,000 memberships
   (LLPs 1023, 1024, 1025). A two-principal slice will pass and teach nothing.
3. **The process is part of the product.** Seventy-one LLPs, over two
   thousand commits, and seven ceiling raises in fourteen days produced a
   sound system nobody can hold in their head. Snapback 3 needs one owner on
   the core, a simulator instead of a test matrix, and budgets anchored to
   competitors so that raising one means admitting a loss.

## 2. What the record says, read as causes

The evidence LLP 2000 §2 cites is right; its reading is too gentle. Every
row below is a Snapback 2 cost recorded in the corpus, with the substrate
that produced it. Numbers are the corpus's own and retain its scope limits.

| Recorded cost | Where | Cause |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Adding `editedAt: time ?` (44 bytes × 244 retained copies = 10,736) pushed `messages.searchAll` to 4,199,688 closure bytes against 4,194,304; the builder shrank a page from 20 to 19 | LLP 1018.008.008; 0.0.21 day-two diary | **Static byte pricing.** Closure bytes are a bound on what a receipt could ship; the product paid for the bound, not the work |
| The 1,000-membership digest fold certifies at 9,922,698 heap and 8,691,704 closure bytes and is refused; the recipient-keyed form is admitted at 2,733,253 / 1,848,418 | LLP 1025 §3 | **Static byte pricing** (heap = steps × 32 + closure scratch). A runtime meter would have run the fold and reported actual bytes |
| A message page is 50, not 200, because a row "prices at 16,016 bytes" and 200 exceed the 1 MiB output ceiling | LLP 1012 §3 | **Static byte pricing** of the worst-case row width |
| A per-recipient search projection is refused at 20,863,641 reserved steps against 1,000,000 | LLP 1024 §1 | **Static step pricing** of a derivation the engine would perform incrementally |
| Full acquisition of 10,008 messages: 67.4 s → 53.6 s → 32.6 s → 28.4 s against a 2–3 s objective; 201 requests, 200 acknowledgement mutations, 612 IndexedDB transactions; PowerSync reports 10,000 rows in 0.36 s native and 2.5 s on web | LLP 1018.008.007; LLP 1018.008.009 §4 | **Result cache, not replica.** Every 50-row page is a certified query, a receipt, a durable acknowledgement mutation, and a store swap |
| Adding a count to a thread makes the composite `partial` offline whenever count authority is missing, so the template declares an independent `history` query and `withHistory` bindings; 27 files / 1,718 lines → 28 / 1,751 after the fix | LLP 1018.008 F2; LLP 1018.008.009 §2 | **Result cache.** The count is not a fact the receipt covers, so the client cannot compose locally |
| `shared/thread.ts` describes table identity, row selection, order, create membership, draft fields and mutation targets a second time | LLP 1018.008 F2 | **Result cache.** Items must be reconstructed from answers because there is no local table to read |
| Sending stalled with 85 IDs remaining against a certified 352-ID slice on an 8,192-ID grant; recovery needed a "Sync now" action | LLP 1018.008 F1 | **Result cache + engine-minted IDs.** Prediction needs server ids before commit, so ids are granted in slices |
| A follower browser tab writes through a durable inbox row; the SDK carries `forwarding`, `writer-unresponsive`, `inbox-full`, `follower` states | LLP 1018.004 | **Result cache.** One writer per partition image; other tabs cannot mutate a receipt-proven image |
| Global search over 1,000 memberships costs ~1,010 HTTP requests through a client N-way merge | LLP 1024 §2 | **Result cache** (no local index over the partition) + **static pricing** (the server-side projection is refused) |
| The complete local runtime grew 7.8 KB → 89,843 gzip bytes; the ceiling was raised 34,000 → 50,000 → 66,000 → 82,000 → 82,500 → 84,000 → 85,000 → 90,000 | LLP 1000 §"Revised"; `rules/NOT-DOING.md` §Moves; LLP 1018.008 §10 | **Result cache.** The inputs list of `metrics/twin.jsonl` is coverage spans, extent facts, held directories, posting trees, fact integrity, swap journals — receipt machinery, not the interpreter |
| `Snapshot` carries `data, next, partial, receipt{state, source, seq, next, reason, rule, sites}, freshness{live, staleSince, connection}, local{predicted, retained, caughtUp, watermark}, refusal, status, reason` | LLP 1018.003 §1 | Both. Every field is a true fact about the mechanism; almost none is a fact about the application |
| `Schema::rule_hash` binds every queued write to the complete schema; an optional column on the subject table invalidates unrelated evidence | LLP 1018.002; LLP 1018.008.008 | **Result cache.** Evidence identity exists because held answers must be re-authorized; a replica re-runs the mutation at the server and needs no evidence identity |

Against that, what scored: on the 2026-09-05 three-scorer Clique run
Snapback 2 beat Convex on authorization (82 vs 79) and offline (81 vs 44)
and lost on queries and live (60 vs 85), client integration (59 vs 79),
setup and docs (55 vs 79), diagnostics and loop (66 vs 77), operations (53
vs 76) and scale (62 vs 80); overall 66 vs 81. The later single-lane 0.0.21
and 0.0.25 scores of 82 and 85 are real improvement and not a three-scorer
comparison. The wins are the core; the losses are composition, discovery,
scale and operations — the substrates and what sits on them.

LLP 2000 §2 summarises this as "costs arise where one layer must reconstruct
another layer's facts." True, and the layer doing the reconstructing is the
client, because it was designed as a cache that may not compute. Name the
cause and the remedy follows.

## 3. Where LLP 2000 is right, and should not be diluted

- **The whole application is the unit.** §3's four properties (composition,
  locality, continuity, performance) are the right acceptance vocabulary.
  Keep them as the headings of the scenario suite.
- **Ownership before abstraction.** §5's table is the best paragraph in the
  document. §8 below turns its fourth row into a fixed vocabulary.
- **Composed reads must not fabricate.** Unknown totals stay unknown; revoked
  rows do not linger. Both flips below preserve this; they change what is
  *known*, not what is *claimed*.
- **Writes have one lifecycle with durable admission distinct from commit
  and stable operation identity.** Keep LLP 1018.004's three outcomes.
- **Rust and SQLite.** Yes, and the engine's transaction, interpreter, rule
  evaluation and successor logic are the pieces to copy first (§5).
- **Prototype the experience before the core.** Yes — and §8 makes the
  prototype's API signatures binding on the implementation, not the reverse.
- **Deterministic simulation early**, citing TigerBeetle. This is the single
  most important sentence in LLP 2000 and §11 makes it the primary harness.
- **A real deployment and a native client early.** Yes; §11 puts the native
  client in slice one.
- **Parallel, fenced, 2000-series, promotion is the author's later call.** Yes.

## 4. Where LLP 2000 is wrong or leaves the wrong things open

### 4.1 It reopens the language and leaves the bound model open; invert that

§6 asks to "evaluate Quarry" and "evaluate any alternative against the same
contracts." The one-language decision took thirty-seven paper applications,
a three-family panel, a spelling spike and an author ruling (LLP
0329.014.004 §9, LLP 1004, LLP 1007 §4b) to close, and the shipped result is
the most readable artifact in Snapback 2: the chat schema is 129 lines and a
query is three to six. Reopening the language will consume the first month.
What *should* be reopened is the bound model under it, which §6 phrases as a
comparison to "concentrate early investigation on." Half of the Quarry
refusals a builder meets are the certifier's byte and step prices wearing
Quarry syntax; the `fn`/`proc` split and the page-to-list gap (LLP 1018.008

import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdirSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { runToolchainCommand } from "./publication-starters.mjs";
import { proveDX5Upgrades } from "./publication-dx5-upgrade.mjs";
import { chromiumExecutable } from "../witness/lib.mjs";

export async function proveDX5(directory, client, platform, expo) {
  const parent=resolve(directory),scratch=mkdtempSync(join(parent,"dx5-"));
  const root=fileURLToPath(new URL("../",import.meta.url)),cli=join(parent,"node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js");
  const env={...process.env,CHROME:chromiumExecutable(),CI:"1",EXPO_NO_TELEMETRY:"1"};delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;delete env.PORT_LO;delete env.PORT_HI;
  const run=(args,cwd=scratch,extra={})=>runToolchainCommand(process.execPath,args,{cwd,env:{...env,...extra},timeout:300000});
  try {
    for(const name of ["clock","diagnostics"]) {
      const cwd=join(scratch,name);mkdirSync(cwd);
      writeFileSync(join(cwd,"consumer.mjs"),readFileSync(join(root,`scripts/dx5-${name}-consumer.mjs`)));
      await run(["consumer.mjs"],cwd);
    }
    await run(["--test",join(root,"packages/snapback4/test/dx5-react.test.mjs")],root,{SNAPBACK4_CLIENT_DIST:join(parent,"node_modules/snapback4/dist")});
    // Retain proof commands and the dependency across an actual init-first adapt.
    const cwd=join(scratch,"adapt");mkdirSync(cwd);
    await run([cli,"init","--expo"],cwd);
    assert.equal(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd,"package.json"))).devDependencies["playwright-core"],"1.55.1");
    assert.match(readFileSync(join(cwd,"metro.config.cjs"),"utf8"),/snapback4-expo\/metro/);
    assert.match(readFileSync(join(cwd,"App.tsx"),"utf8"),/snapback4-expo\/native-smoke/);
    writeFileSync(join(cwd,"snapback/schema.q"),"use identity\nquery alive():\n  return true\n");
    await run([cli,"init","--adapt","--apply"],cwd);
    assert.match(readFileSync(join(cwd,"scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"),"utf8"),/E_FRONTEND_SCENARIO/);
    const pkg=JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd,"package.json"))),scripts=pkg.scripts;
    assert.equal(scripts["frontend:proof"],"node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");assert.equal(scripts["proof:web"],scripts["frontend:proof"]);
    pkg.dependencies.snapback4="file:"+resolve(client);pkg.dependencies["snapback4-expo"]="file:"+resolve(expo);
    pkg.optionalDependencies={"snapback4-linux-x64":"file:"+resolve(platform)};
    writeFileSync(join(cwd,"package.json"),JSON.stringify(pkg));
    await runToolchainCommand("npm",["install","--prefer-offline","--no-audit","--no-fund"],{cwd,env,timeout:300000});
    await run([cli,"check"],cwd);
    await runToolchainCommand("npm",["run","typecheck"],{cwd,env,timeout:300000});
    writeFileSync(join(cwd,"expected-refusal.mjs"),`import assert from 'node:assert/strict';import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
const r=spawnSync(process.execPath,['scripts/frontend-proof.mjs'],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:240000,maxBuffer:8<<20});
assert.equal(r.status,1,r.stdout+r.stderr);assert.match(r.stderr,/E_FRONTEND_SCENARIO: frontend rendered/);console.log('DX5 adapted proof ran as written and refused the missing write scenario');`);
    await run(["expected-refusal.mjs"],cwd);
    // Public custom schemas have no sign-in control; their proof still renders.
    writeFileSync(join(cwd,"snapback/schema.q"),"query alive():\n  return true\n");
    await run([cli,"init","--adapt","--apply"],cwd);
    await run([cli,"check"],cwd);
    await run(["expected-refusal.mjs"],cwd);
    const upgrades=await proveDX5Upgrades(client,platform);
    return {status:"passed",clock:"reused store fired/held/recovery",diagnostics:"source advice and wire privacy",readState:"verbatim packed guide snippet, types and all states",starter:"packaged Metro/smoke, Playwright, retained proof",upgrades};
  } finally {rmSync(scratch,{recursive:true,force:true});}
}

+            let after = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
+            let fired: Vec<_> = engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().filter(|(i, _)| {
+                matches!((before.get(*i).copied().flatten(), after.get(*i).copied().flatten()), (Some(old), Some(new)) if new > old)
+            }).map(|(_, schedule)| schedule.name.clone()).collect();
+            let mut schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
+            for (index, schedule) in schedules.iter_mut().enumerate() {
+                if schedule["fired"] != true && !shared.hold_schedules && before.get(index).copied().flatten().is_some_and(|old| old < engine.schema().schedules[index].boundary(now)) {
+                    schedule["holdCode"] = "maintenance-failed".into();
+                    schedule["reason"] = "clock advanced but maintenance stopped before this due boundary was queued; inspect clock and queued jobs before continuing; do not replay the advance".into();
+                }
+            }
+            let (fired, held) = crate::dev_clock_store::outcomes(&schedules);
+            respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "sessionNotice":crate::dev_clock::SESSION_NOTICE, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "schedules":schedules, "fired":fired, "held":held, "reportIncomplete":true, "denied":refusal_json(&error)}))
+        },
     }
 }

+// Metro resolves the consumer's Expo toolchain, including isolated installs.
+const { createRequire } = require("node:module");
+const { join } = require("node:path");
+exports.transform = function (args) {
+  const requireApp = createRequire(join(args.options.projectRoot, "package.json"));
+  const expoRequire = createRequire(requireApp.resolve("expo/metro-config"));
+  const upstream = expoRequire("@expo/metro-config/babel-transformer");
+  const src = args.options.platform === "web"
+    ? args.src.replace(/new URL\(("\.\/wasm\/snapback4_device_bg\.wasm"), import\.meta\.url\)/g, "require($1)")
+    : args.src;
+  return upstream.transform({ ...args, src });
+};

+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — sign-in is confined to the owned native prime launch.
+import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
+import { useAuth } from "snapback4/react";
+import { smokeAccount, smokeSegment } from "./witness";
+
+/** Ordinary launches do nothing. Password apps explicitly opt into the
+ * driver's disposable account; guest apps keep ordinary guest sign-in. */
+export function useNativeSmoke(password = false) {
+  const auth = useAuth(), attempted = useRef(false);
+  useEffect(() => {
+    if (smokeSegment() !== "prime" || auth.state !== "signed-out" || attempted.current) return;
+    const account = password ? smokeAccount() : null;
+    if (password && !account) return;
+    attempted.current = true;
+    if (account) void auth.signup(account.email, account.password);
+    else void auth.guest();
+  }, [auth, password]);
+}

+/** Render a list read or page in one expression. Empty describes returned
+ * rows, including partial/stale answers; it never proves absence. This owns
+ * no observation and does not retain a previous read. Native callers supply
+ * Text/View elements in the same slots. A missing denied renderer throws the
+ * original typed QueryRefusal for an error boundary. */
+// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — presentation preserves precise read evidence.
+export function ReadState<R extends readonly unknown[]>({ read: source, loading = null, empty = null, denied, children }: {
+  read: Read<R> | { read: Read<R> };
+  loading?: ReactNode;
+  empty?: ReactNode;
+  denied?: (why: Refusal) => ReactNode;
+  children: (items: R, read: Ready<R>) => ReactNode;
+}): ReactNode {
+  const read = "read" in source ? source.read : source;
+  if ("denied" in read) {
+    if (denied) return denied(read.denied);
+    throw new QueryRefusal(read.denied);
+  }
+  if (!("data" in read)) return loading;
+  return read.data.length === 0 ? empty : children(read.data, read);
+}

use serde_json::{Value, json};
use super::{Options, pretty};

const README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend proofs\n\n`npm run frontend:proof` builds its own dist, then proves the starter in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\nDay-two scaffolding is opt-in: `snapback4 init --adapt --day2 --apply` captures\ncurrent sources and fixtures in versions/v1 and versions/v2 and adds day2:proof.\nReplace its BUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN block before running `npm run day2:proof`.\nSet PORT_LO and PORT_HI at run time to confine proof servers and launchers.\nSet CHROME=/path/to/chromium or install Playwright's Chromium.\n";

const PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS: &str = "\n## Frontend and day-two proofs\n\nAfter `npm run build` (web) or `npm run build:web` (Expo), run\n`npm run frontend:proof` to prove sign-in and the starter form in Chromium.\nAfter replacing the screen, add your business assertions to scripts/frontend-proof.mjs.\n`npm run day2:proof -- versions/v1.q versions/v2.q` proves saved-source deploy,\nrollback and compatible forward without replacing your working schema.\nSet `PORT_LO` and `PORT_HI` at run time to confine proof servers and launchers;\nunset means allocated proof ports and the interactive starter defaults.\nSet `CHROME=/path/to/chromium` or install Playwright's Chromium (`npx playwright-core install chromium`).\n";
/// Only the exact generated appendix is ignored for historical byte recognition.
pub(super) fn without_readme(text: &str) -> &str { text.strip_suffix(README_PROOFS).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_30_README_PROOFS)).or_else(|| text.strip_suffix(PREVIOUS_README_PROOFS)).unwrap_or(text) }
pub(super) fn readme(mut text: String) -> String {
    if !text.contains("`npm run frontend:proof`") { text.push_str(README_PROOFS); }
    text
}

pub(super) fn files(files: &mut Vec<(&'static str, String)>, options: &Options, backend: u16, frontend: u16, web: bool, shape: &super::starter::Shape) {
    let has_frontend = true;
    let has_write_scenario = shape.body == super::starter::Body::Todos || options.flags.contains_key("template");
    for (_, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "README.md") {
        if has_frontend { *text = readme(std::mem::take(text)); }
    }
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut() {
        if *name == "vite.config.mts" { *text = runtime_vite(text, backend, frontend); }
    }
    let proof = if has_write_scenario { frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), options.flags.get("template").map(String::as_str), web) } else { frontend_scaffold(web) };
    let dir = options.positionals.first().map(std::path::Path::new).unwrap_or_else(|| std::path::Path::new("."));
    let sources = if let Ok(Some(own)) = super::own_schema(dir) {
        own.files.iter().filter_map(|name| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("snapback").join(name)).ok().map(|text| (name.clone(), text))).collect::<Vec<_>>()
    } else { files.iter().find(|(name, _)| *name == "snapback/schema.q").map(|(_, text)| vec![("schema.q".to_owned(), text.clone())]).unwrap_or_default() };
    let day2 = options.flags.contains_key("day2");
    for version in ["v1", "v2"].into_iter().filter(|_| day2) {
        if dir.join("versions").join(version).exists() { continue; }
        // Init is a one-shot CLI operation. Destinations live for this invocation's
        // plan, whose existing interface uses static generated names.
        for (name, text) in &sources {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text.clone()));
        }
        let marker: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/.gitkeep").into_boxed_str());
        files.push((marker, String::new()));
        let mut fixtures = files.iter().filter_map(|(name, text)| name.strip_prefix("fixtures/").map(|name| (name.to_owned(), text.clone()))).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if fixtures.is_empty() {
            if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("fixtures")) {
                for entry in entries.flatten().filter(|entry| entry.path().extension().is_some_and(|ext| ext == "jsonl") || entry.file_name() == "test.sweep.json") {
                    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(entry.path()) { fixtures.push((entry.file_name().to_string_lossy().into_owned(), text)); }
                }
            }
        }
        for (name, text) in fixtures {
            let path: &'static str = Box::leak(format!("versions/{version}/fixtures/{name}").into_boxed_str());
            files.push((path, text));
        }
    }
    if has_frontend { files.push(("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", proof)); }
    if day2 { files.push(("scripts/day2-proof.mjs", include_str!("day2-proof.mjs").to_owned())); }
    files.push(("scripts/launch.mjs", include_str!("launch.mjs").replace("BACKEND", &backend.to_string()).replace("FRONTEND", &frontend.to_string())));
    for (name, text) in files.iter_mut().filter(|(name, _)| *name == "package.json") {
        let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).unwrap();
        if has_frontend { package["scripts"]["frontend:proof"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"); }
        if day2 { package["scripts"]["day2:proof"] = json!("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"); }
        if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
            package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
        }
        if has_frontend {
            package["scripts"]["proof:web"] = json!("node scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
            package["devDependencies"]["playwright-core"] = json!("1.55.1");
        }
        package["scripts"]["backend"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs backend");
        if web {
            package["scripts"]["dev"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite");
            package["scripts"]["preview"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs vite-preview");
        } else {
            package["scripts"]["start"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo");
            package["scripts"]["web"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs expo-web");
            package["scripts"]["native:smoke"] = json!("node scripts/launch.mjs native-smoke");
        }
        *text = pretty(&package);
        let _ = name;
    }
}

pub(super) fn frontend_proof(source: &str, template: Option<&str>, web: bool) -> String {
    // Social keeps account controls on its Profile tab; prove the actual route.
    let mut proof = source.replace("// TEMPLATE_READY", if template == Some("social") {
        "await page.getByText(\"Profile\", { exact: true }).click();"
    } else { "" });
    if template == Some("social") {
        proof = proof.replace("getByLabel(\"Task\"", "getByLabel(\"Display name\"")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Save profile\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"saveProfile\", { name: title }");
    } else if template == Some("chat") {
        proof = proof.replace("    const task =", r#"    const viewer = await page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.viewer());
    const seeded = await fetch(`${dev.url}/seed`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify([
      { table: "conversations", row: { id: "proof-room", title: "Proof room", creatorId: viewer } },
      { table: "memberships", row: { id: "proof-member", conversationId: "proof-room", principal: viewer, role: "admin", joinedAt: Date.now() } }
    ]) });
    assert.ok(seeded.ok, await seeded.text());
    await page.getByText("Proof room", { exact: true }).click();
    const task ="#)
            .replace("page.getByLabel(\"Task\", { exact: true })", "page.getByPlaceholder(\"Message\", { exact: true })")
            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Send\"")
            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"send\", { conversationId: \"proof-room\", body: title }");
        // Chat receipts echo drafts. Restrict evidence to its message display.
        if web { proof = proof.replace("renderedText(page, new RegExp(title))", "renderedText(page, new RegExp(title), { within: page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" }) })"); }
    }
    let scope = match (template, web) {
        (Some("social"), _) => "page.getByTestId(\"profile-results\")",
        (Some("chat"), true) => "page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" })",
        (Some("chat"), false) => "page.getByTestId(\"thread-results\")",
        (_, true) => "page.getByRole(\"list\")",
        (_, false) => "page.getByTestId(\"todo-results\")",
    };
    proof = proof.replace("RESULTS_SCOPE", scope);
    proof = proof.replace("BUILD_SCRIPT", if web { "build" } else { "build:web" });
    proof
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — a rendered starter is not an application write proof.
pub(super) fn frontend_scaffold(web: bool) -> String {
    let proof = frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), None, web);
    let (setup, _) = proof.split_once("    const task =").expect("frontend proof's write section");
    // A public custom schema need not invent identity just to prove rendering.
    let setup = setup.replace(r#"} else {
      assert.ok(personas.length, "declare identity or personas before proving the starter");"#, "} else if (personas.length) {")
        .replace(r#"    await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^(Sign out|Switch persona)$/, exact: true }).waitFor();"#, r#"    if (identity.guests || identity.password || personas.length) await page.getByRole("button", { name: /^(Sign out|Switch persona)$/, exact: true }).waitFor();"#);
    format!("{setup}    assert.ok((await page.locator('body').innerText()).trim(), 'frontend rendered no content');\n    // Add a real UI write and assert its SDK receipt and fresh rendered result here.\n    throw Error('E_FRONTEND_SCENARIO: frontend rendered; no app-specific write scenario yet. Edit scripts/frontend-proof.mjs and replace this refusal with your write assertions.');\n  }});\n}} finally {{ await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }}\n")
}

fn previous_frontend(dir: &std::path::Path, web: bool) -> Option<Option<&'static str>> {
    super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).filter(|(version,_)| matches!(*version,"0.4.28"|"0.4.29"|"0.4.30"|"0.4.31"|"0.4.32")).map(|(_,template)|template)
}

/// Adapt adds missing proof infrastructure, preserving files and script names
/// already owned by the application. It never runs a package manager.
pub(super) fn plan(dir: &std::path::Path, own: &super::OwnSchema, enable_day2: bool) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
    let web = dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file();
    let router = dir.join("app/_layout.tsx").is_file();
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let shape = super::starter::Shape { title: super::title(dir), password: own.identity.is_some_and(|(p, g)| p && !g),
        personas: own.identity.is_none().then(|| own.personas.clone()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()),
        body: if super::adapt::fits_todos(own) { super::starter::Body::Todos } else { super::starter::Body::Home(None) }, offline: own.offline.clone() };
    let path = dir.join("package.json");
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;
    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
    let router = router && !web && package["main"] == "expo-router/entry";
    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
    let original = package.clone();
    let day2_text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")).ok();
    let referenced_day2 = package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v| v.as_str().is_some_and(|s| s.contains("scripts/day2-proof.mjs"))));
    let missing_managed_day2 = day2_text.is_none() && matches!(package["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str(),
        Some("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"));
    let managed_day2 = day2_text.as_deref().is_some_and(super::proof_advice::known_day2) || missing_managed_day2;
    let day2 = enable_day2 || referenced_day2 || package.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") != Some(&json!(false)) && managed_day2;
    // Deleting both the command and file records a durable opt-out on apply.
    // Preview/check never write the inferred choice. Explicit --day2 opts in again.
    if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
        package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
    }
    let backend = package["scripts"]["backend"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ")).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok())
.or_else(|| launcher_port(dir, "backend")).unwrap_or(4400);
    let frontend = launcher_port(dir, "frontend").or_else(|| package["scripts"][if web { "dev" } else { "start" }].as_str()
        .and_then(|s| s.split_once("--port ")).and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split_whitespace().next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())
        .or_else(|| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")).ok().and_then(|s| s.split_once("server: { port: ").and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())))
        ).unwrap_or(if backend != 4400 { backend.saturating_add(1) } else if web { 5173 } else { 8081 });
    let mut generated = vec![("package.json", pretty(&package))];
    let mut flags = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    if router { flags.insert("template".into(), "social".into()); }
    else if let Some(Some(template)) = previous_frontend(dir, web) { flags.insert("template".into(), template.into()); }
    if day2 { flags.insert("day2".into(), String::new()); }
    files(&mut generated, &Options { positionals: vec![dir.to_string_lossy().into_owned()], flags, json: false }, backend, frontend, web, &shape);
    // A saved version is immutable application evidence. Never fill its missing
    // fixture files from today's working release during an adaptation.
    generated.retain(|(name, _)| !name.starts_with("versions/") || {
        let version = name.split('/').nth(1).unwrap();
        !dir.join("versions").join(version).exists()
    });
    // New directories capture the current source and contemporary fixtures. Flat
    // legacy snapshots remain untouched evidence; they are never propagated.
    // Custom proofs own their saved source layout; do not manufacture snapshots.
    if !managed_day2 && !enable_day2 { generated.retain(|(name,_)| !name.starts_with("versions/")); }
    let wanted: Value = serde_json::from_str(&generated[0].1).unwrap();
    for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
        if !package[group].is_object() { package[group] = json!({}); }
        for (key, value) in wanted[group].as_object().unwrap() {
            let old = package[group].get(key).and_then(Value::as_str);
            let managed = group == "scripts" && old.is_some_and(|old| match key.as_str() {
                "day2:proof" => managed_day2 && matches!(old, "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q"),
                "backend" => old.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ").is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                "dev" | "preview" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "dev" { "vite --port " } else { "vite preview --port " }).and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(" --strictPort")).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                "start" | "web" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "start" { "expo start --port " } else { "expo start --web --port " }).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
                _ => false,
            });
            if managed || ((key == "day2:proof" && enable_day2 || key == "frontend:proof" && super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || key == "playwright-core" && (super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || super::proof_advice::referenced(dir, "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"))) && old.is_none()) { package[group][key] = value.clone(); }
        }
    }
    let mut config_changes = Vec::new();
    if web {
        if let Ok(current) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")) {
            // Replace only the exact initializer forms, allowing their port literals.
            let numbers = current.split("port: ").skip(1).filter_map(|s| s.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()?.parse::<u16>().ok()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let mut normalized = current.clone();
            for number in &numbers { normalized = normalized.replace(&format!("port: {number}"), "port: 5173"); }
            let reference = include_str!("starter/web/vite.config.mts").replace("port: 4173", "port: 5173");
            if normalized == reference { config_changes.push(("vite.config.mts", runtime_vite(&current, backend, numbers.first().copied().unwrap_or(5173)))); }
        }
    }
    let inherited_frontend = previous_frontend(dir, web).filter(|template| super::adapt::fits_todos(own) || template.is_some());
    if let Some(template) = inherited_frontend {
        if let Some((_, text)) = generated.iter_mut().find(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs") {
            *text = frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), template, web);
        }
    }
    let referenced_after = |path: &str| package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))));
    let mut additions: Vec<_> = generated.into_iter().skip(1)
        .filter(|(name, _)| {
            if *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" { return inherited_frontend.is_some() || !dir.join(name).exists() && referenced_after(name); }
            if *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { return day2 && (managed_day2 || !dir.join(name).exists() && (enable_day2 || referenced_day2)); }
            if !dir.join(name).exists() {
                return name.starts_with("versions/") || referenced_after(name);
            }
            false
        }).collect();
    additions.extend(config_changes);
    if package != original { additions.push(("package.json", pretty(&package))); }
    Ok(additions)
}

fn launcher_port(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/launch.mjs")).ok()?;
    // Recognize the generated fallback expression, not client application code.
    let marker = if name == "backend" { "low === null ? " } else { ": low === null ? " };
    text.split_once(marker)?.1.split_whitespace().next()?.parse().ok()
}


pub(super) fn apply(dir: &std::path::Path, additions: &[(&str, String)]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    for (name, body) in additions {
        let path = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap())?;
        if *name == "package.json" || *name == "vite.config.mts" || *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" || *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { super::replace_file(&path, body)?; }
        else {
            use std::io::Write;
            let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(&path)?;
            file.write_all(body.as_bytes())?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Environment is evaluated whenever Vite loads the config, never at init time.
pub(super) fn runtime_vite(text: &str, backend: u16, frontend: u16) -> String {
    if text.contains("const proofRange") { return text.to_owned(); }
    let header = format!(r#"const proofRange = [process.env.PORT_LO, process.env.PORT_HI];
if (proofRange.some(v => v !== undefined) && (!proofRange.every(v => v && /^\d+$/.test(v)) || Number(proofRange[0]) < 1 || Number(proofRange[1]) > 65535 || Number(proofRange[0]) >= Number(proofRange[1]))) throw Error("PORT_LO/PORT_HI must provide at least two valid ports");
const backendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {backend} : Number(proofRange[0]);
const frontendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {frontend} : backendPort + 1;
process.env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL ??= `http://127.0.0.1:${{backendPort}}`;
"#);
    let mut result = text.to_owned();
    // All generated configurations have literal ports; preserve unrelated config.
    for port in [frontend, 5173, 4173] { result = result.replace(&format!("port: {port}"), "port: frontendPort"); }
    format!("{header}{result}")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn historical_frontend_templates_keep_assertions_and_gain_their_own_build() {
        for (version, source) in [
            (28, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.28.mjs")),
            (29, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.29.mjs")),
            (30, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.30.mjs")),
            (31, include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.31.mjs")),
        ] {
            for web in [false, true] {
                for template in [None, Some("chat"), Some("social")] {
                    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("day3-historical-proof-{}-{version}-{web}-{template:?}", std::process::id()));
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("snapback")).unwrap();
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join("scripts")).unwrap();
                    let screen = if web { "src/App.tsx" } else { "App.tsx" };
                    std::fs::create_dir_all(dir.join(screen).parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join(screen), "// screen").unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join("package.json"), r#"{"scripts":{}}"#).unwrap();
                    std::fs::write(dir.join("snapback/schema.q"), include_str!("starter/schema.q")).unwrap();
                    let path = dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs");
                    std::fs::write(&path, frontend_proof(source, template, web)).unwrap();
                    let own = super::super::own_schema(&dir).unwrap().unwrap();
                    let changes = plan(&dir, &own, false).unwrap();
                    let updated = &changes.iter().find(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs").unwrap().1;
                    assert_eq!(updated, &frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), template, web));
                    assert!(updated.contains(if web { "[\"run\", \"build\"]" } else { "[\"run\", \"build:web\"]" }));
                    if template == Some("social") { assert!(updated.contains("Save profile") && updated.contains("getByText(\"Profile\"")); }
                    if template == Some("chat") { assert!(updated.contains("Proof room") && updated.contains("name: \"Send\"")); }
                    std::fs::write(path, format!("{}\n// app-owned assertion\n", frontend_proof(source, template, web))).unwrap();
                    assert!(!plan(&dir, &own, false).unwrap().iter().any(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"));
                    std::fs::remove_dir_all(dir).unwrap();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
//! Advice is evidence about known template bytes, or explicitly a heuristic.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — custom proofs own their arguments and assertions.
use std::path::Path;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use serde::Deserialize;

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Template { file: String, version: String, template: Option<String>, web: bool, sha256: String }
fn templates() -> &'static [Template] {
    static TEMPLATES: std::sync::OnceLock<Vec<Template>> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    TEMPLATES.get_or_init(|| serde_json::from_str(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).expect("shipped proof digests"))
}
pub(super) fn digest(text: &str) -> String { format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(text.as_bytes())) }
pub(super) fn known_day2(text: &str) -> bool {
    let hash = digest(text);
    templates().iter().any(|t| t.file == "day2" && t.sha256 == hash)
}
/// Exact shipped helper bytes identify its identity mode and starter revision.
pub(super) fn native_template(text: &str) -> Option<(&'static str, bool)> {
    let hash = digest(text);
    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "native" && t.sha256 == hash)
        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref() == Some("password")))
}
pub(super) fn frontend_template(dir: &Path, web: bool) -> Option<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>)> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")).ok()?;
    let hash = digest(&text);
    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "frontend" && t.web == web && t.sha256 == hash)
        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref()))
}
pub(super) fn known_frontend(dir: &Path) -> bool { [false,true].into_iter().any(|web|frontend_template(dir,web).is_some()) }
fn package(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}
pub(super) fn referenced(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> bool {
    package(dir)["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))))
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#15-wave-15-starter-advice-day5-2026-10-18 — compare the complete directory capture; no distinguished schema.q is required.
pub(super) fn legacy_differences(dir: &Path, plan: &[(&str, String)]) -> Vec<String> {
    ["v1", "v2"].into_iter().filter_map(|version| {
        let flat = format!("versions/{version}.q");
        let prefix = format!("versions/{version}/");
        let legacy = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(&flat)).ok()?;
        let mut saved = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join(&prefix)) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let path = entry.path();
                if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext|ext=="q") && let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                    saved.insert(format!("{prefix}{}",entry.file_name().to_string_lossy()),text);
                }
            }
        }
        for (name,text) in plan.iter().filter(|(name,_)|name.starts_with(&prefix) && name.ends_with(".q")) {
            saved.insert((*name).to_owned(),text.clone());
        }
        if saved.is_empty() || saved.len()==1 && saved.values().next()==Some(&legacy) {return None;}
        let paths=saved.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        Some(format!("info: {flat} differs from {paths}; saved {version} captures snapback/*.q, not the legacy flat source; both remain unchanged evidence. Review the pair before day2:proof"))
    }).collect()
}
pub(super) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut advice = legacy_differences(dir, &[]);
    if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("versions")) {
        let mut paths = Vec::new();
        for entry in entries.flatten() {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_dir() {
                if let Ok(files) = std::fs::read_dir(&path) { paths.extend(files.flatten().map(|entry|entry.path()).filter(|path|path.extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="q"))); }
            } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="q") { paths.push(path); }
        }
        paths.sort();
        for path in paths {
            if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                let hash = digest(&text);
                if let Some(t) = templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "todo-schema" && t.sha256 == hash) {
                    advice.push(format!("info: {} is the unmodified {} todo snapshot (sha256 {hash}); replace it with this app's saved schema and contemporary fixtures before day2:proof", path.strip_prefix(dir).unwrap_or(&path).display(), t.version));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/launch.mjs"] {
        if !dir.join(path).exists() && referenced(dir, path) {
            advice.push(format!("info: {path} is missing but package.json still references it; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`, then `snapback4 init --adapt --apply`"));
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")) {
        let hash=digest(&text);
        if let Some(t)=templates().iter().find(|t| t.file=="day2" && t.sha256==hash) {
            if matches!(t.version.as_str(),"0.4.28"|"0.4.29") {
                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} starter: it asserts no business rows; preview `snapback4 init --adapt` to replace it and capture current sources/fixtures",t.version));
            }
            if text.contains("assert.fail(") {
                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} assert.fail business-assertion placeholder (sha256 {hash}); replace BUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN with this app's persisted-row and refusal assertions", t.version));
            }
            if t.version=="0.4.30" || t.version=="0.4.31" {
                let script=package(dir)["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
                if script=="node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" {
                    advice.push("info: the unmodified day-two starter needs its two saved-source arguments; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`".into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")) {
        // Custom JavaScript can hide builds in arbitrary imports/functions. These
        // checks deliberately make no claim about its behavior or ownership.
        let active=text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let flat=active.replace(['\"','\'',',','[',']','(',')'], " ");
        let words=flat.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let serves_dist=words.windows(2).any(|w|w==["startStatic","dist"]);
        let pkg=package(dir);
        let launch=pkg["scripts"]["frontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let pre=pkg["scripts"]["prefrontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let builds=|s:&str|s.contains("build")||s.contains("export");
        if serves_dist && !builds(&active) && !builds(launch) && !builds(pre) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs serves dist with no visible build step (heuristic; imported code may build it); build in the proof or its npm script before startStatic(\"dist\")".into());
        }
        if words.windows(3).any(|w|w==["npx","snapback4","dev"]) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs launches npx snapback4 dev (heuristic); use startDev from snapback4/proof for an owned server and bounded cleanup".into());
        }
    }
    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/proof.mjs", "scripts/acceptance.mjs"] {
        let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(path)) else { continue; };
        let active = text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let npx = active.contains("\"npx\"") || active.contains("'npx'");
        let status_only = active.contains("/auth/config") && (active.contains("status === 200") || active.contains("status == 200") || active.contains("[200, 404]") || active.contains("status === 404"));
        if npx && status_only {
            advice.push(format!("info: {path} spawns npx and accepts readiness HTTP status (heuristic; sha256 {}); readiness is not business evidence. Use snapback4/proof hosts and assert persisted rows plus refusals at each release", digest(&text)));
        }
    }
    advice
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn every_shipped_proof_template_has_a_digest() {
        let mut expected=Vec::new();
        for (version,frontend,day2) in [
            ("0.4.28",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.28.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.28.mjs")),
            ("0.4.29",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.29.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.29.mjs")),
            ("0.4.30",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.30.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.30.mjs")),
            ("0.4.31",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.31.mjs"),include_str!("day2-proof.mjs")),
            ("0.4.32",include_str!("previous/frontend-proof-0.4.32.mjs"),include_str!("previous/day2-proof-0.4.32.mjs")),
            ("0.4.33",include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"),include_str!("day2-proof.mjs")),
        ] {
            for web in [false,true] { for template in [None,Some("chat"),Some("social")] {
                expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"frontend","version":version,"web":web,"template":template,"sha256":digest(&super::super::proof::frontend_proof(frontend,template,web))}));
            }}
            expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"day2","version":version,"web":false,"template":null,"sha256":digest(day2)}));
        }
        for (version, source) in [
            ("0.4.9", include_str!("starter/previous/native-smoke-0.4.9.ts")),
            ("0.4.16", include_str!("starter/previous/native-smoke-0.4.16.ts")),
            ("0.4.28", include_str!("starter/previous/wave14/native-smoke.ts")),
            ("0.4.31", include_str!("native-smoke.ts")),
        ] {
            for password in [false, true].into_iter().filter(|password| !*password || version != "0.4.9") {
                let text = super::super::starter::native_smoke_version(password, source);
                let version = if version == "0.4.16" && password { "0.4.25" } else { version };
                expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"native","version":version,"web":false,"template":if password { Some("password") } else { None },"sha256":digest(&text)}));
            }
        }
        expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"todo-schema","version":"0.4.30","web":false,"template":null,"sha256":digest(&super::super::without_design_references(include_str!("starter/previous/schema-0.4.30.q")))}));
        expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"todo-schema","version":"0.4.33","web":false,"template":null,"sha256":digest(&super::super::without_design_references(include_str!("starter/previous/schema-0.4.33.q")))}));
        expected.push(serde_json::json!({"file":"todo-schema","version":"0.4.35","web":false,"template":null,"sha256":digest(&super::super::without_design_references(include_str!("starter/schema.q")))}));
        if std::env::var_os("SNAPBACK4_UPDATE_PROOF_DIGESTS").is_some() {
            std::fs::write(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),"/src/verbs/init/proof-digests.json"),serde_json::to_string_pretty(&expected).unwrap()+"\n").unwrap();
        } else {
            assert_eq!(serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).unwrap(),serde_json::json!(expected),"retain historical digests and register each new shipped template");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn readiness_npx_advice_is_heuristic_and_does_not_claim_template_ownership() {
    let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-day5-proof-advice-{}",std::process::id()));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("scripts")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(root.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"), "spawn('npx', ['snapback4','dev']); fetch('/auth/config'); if (res.ok || res.status === 404) return;").unwrap();
    let advice=report(&root).join("\n");
    assert!(advice.contains("spawns npx and accepts readiness HTTP status (heuristic; sha256"),"{advice}");
    assert!(!known_frontend(&root));
    std::fs::remove_dir_all(root).unwrap();
}

// Replace the application assertions when replacing the starter screen.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";

// For SVG/foreignObject controls, click the actual rendered box, not a text
// descendant whose DOM geometry Playwright may interpret differently.
async function clickSvg(page, control) {
  await control.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  const box = await control.boundingBox();
  assert.ok(box && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0, "SVG control has no rendered box");
  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
}

const range = portRange();
if (range !== "0" && range.split("-")[0] === range.split("-")[1]) throw Error("the starter needs two runtime ports");

assert.equal(build.code, 0, `frontend:proof build failed: ${build.stderr}; logs: ${build.logs.stderr}`);

  const personas = (await (await fetch(`${dev.url}/dev/status`, { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(5000) })).json()).personas;

      await button.evaluate((element, title) => window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm("add", { title }, element), title);

      const receipt = await bounded(page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.formResult()), 30_000, "clicked write receipt");
      assert.equal(receipt.state, "sent", JSON.stringify(receipt));
      const within = RESULTS_SCOPE;
      await waitForFresh(renderedText(page, new RegExp(title), { within }), null);
    }
  });
  console.log("frontend:proof passed");
} finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — bounded candidate scan, never an automatic script rewrite.
// Project-specific migration advice. Read authored files only; never print their contents.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, lstatSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
const excluded = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', '.snapback4', 'generated', 'dist', 'build', 'target', 'deploy', 'logs', 'store', 'output', '.runtime']);
export function upgradeWarnings(root, declarations) {
  const doors = [], persistence = [], dataJson = [];
  let scanned = 0, incomplete = false;
  const walk = (dir, prefix = '', depth = 0) => {
    if (scanned > 10000 || depth > 8) { incomplete = true; return; }
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(dir, {withFileTypes: true}); } catch { incomplete = true; return; }
    for (const entry of entries.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
      if (++scanned > 10000) { incomplete = true; return; }
      if (excluded.has(entry.name) || entry.name.startsWith('.')) continue;
      const relative = prefix + entry.name, path = join(dir, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) { walk(path, relative + '/', depth + 1); continue; }
      if (!entry.isFile() || !/\.(?:[cm]?[jt]sx?|sh|json)$/.test(entry.name)) continue;
      let size; try { size = lstatSync(path).size; } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      if (size > 1048576) { incomplete = true; continue; }
      let source; try { source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8'); } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      if (/\/seed\b|\/dev\/|x-snapback-persona/.test(source)) doors.push(relative);
      if (/\bstartDev\s*\(/.test(source)) persistence.push(relative);
      // Candidate scan: shell commands, argv arrays and npm-script strings.
      // Never infer how the parsed result is used or rewrite application code.
      const commandText = source.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      if (/(?:\bsnapback4(?:@[^\s"']+)?\s+data\b|["']data(?:["']|\s))[^;]{0,2000}--json/.test(commandText)
        || /(?:\bsnapback4\b[^;]{0,2000}--json[^;]{0,2000}\bdata\b|["']--json["'][^;]{0,2000}["']data["'])/.test(commandText)) dataJson.push(relative);
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  const warnings = [];
  const passwordOnly = /^\s*use[ \t]+identity[ \t]+password\b/m.test(declarations) && !/^\s*use[ \t]+identity(?:[ \t]*(?:--[^\n]*)?)?$/m.test(declarations);
  if (passwordOnly) warnings.push('password-only dev doors are CLOSED since 0.4.33: /seed, /dev/* and persona headers refuse. Use deploy staging --staging plus serve staging for real users (guide staging); local fixtures explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors.' + (doors.length ? ' Review: ' + doors.join(', ') : ' No matching dev-door scripts found.'));
  if (persistence.length) warnings.push('startDev() persists since 0.4.33: reruns see old rows and share the dev store. For disposable acceptance use startDev({args:["--memory"]}); keep persistence for continuity. Review (check each call for explicit memory): ' + persistence.join(', '));
  if (dataJson.length) warnings.push('data --json returns {rows, more, limit} since 0.4.35, replacing the bare array. One-line fix: JSON.parse(stdout).rows (was JSON.parse(stdout)); use more/limit to detect truncation. Review candidate consumers: ' + dataJson.join(', '));
  if (incomplete) warnings.push('migration scan incomplete (unreadable/large files or depth/file limit); inspect remaining authored scripts for /seed, /dev/*, persona headers, startDev() and data --json array consumers.');
  return warnings;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — the OLD CLI invokes the NEW wrapper for its post-upgrade check.
export function firstCheckWarnings(cwd = process.cwd()) {
  if (process.env.SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_BASELINE === '1') return;
  let temporary;
  try {
    let root = cwd;
    while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) return; root = parent; }
    const schema = join(root, 'snapback');
    if (!existsSync(schema)) return;
    const version = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url)), 'utf8')).version;
    const receipt = join(root, '.snapback4/project-warnings-version.json');
    try { if (JSON.parse(readFileSync(receipt, 'utf8')).version === version) return; } catch { /* First check or unreadable receipt: deliver. */ }
    const declarations = readdirSync(schema).filter(name => name.endsWith('.q')).map(name => readFileSync(join(schema, name), 'utf8')).join('\n');
    const warnings = upgradeWarnings(root, declarations);
    const name = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8')).name ?? root;
    for (const warning of warnings) process.stderr.write(`project ${name}: upgrade warning: ${warning}\n`);
    // Old upgrade --quiet captures and discards check stderr. A pipe is not
    // evidence of delivery: leave advice pending for the next visible check.
    if (warnings.length && !process.stderr.isTTY) return;
    mkdirSync(dirname(receipt), {recursive:true});
    temporary = `${receipt}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify({version}) + '\n', {flag:'wx'});
    renameSync(temporary, receipt);
  } catch {
    process.stderr.write('snapback4: project migration advice unavailable or not acknowledged; check will continue. Review guide changes and run upgrade to scan authored scripts.\n');
  } finally { if (temporary) try { rmSync(temporary, {force:true}); } catch { /* Advisory only. */ } }
}
// Real old executables perform acquisition; only candidate package metadata is substituted.
import assert from "node:assert/strict";
import { createServer } from "node:http";
import { mkdtempSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { spawnSync } from "node:child_process";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import { runToolchainCommand } from "./publication-starters.mjs";

export async function proveDX5Upgrades(client, platform) {
  const scratch=mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(),"sb4-dx5-upgrade-")), artifacts=[], results=[];
  let registry;
  try {
  const sync=(cmd,args,options={})=>{
    const r=spawnSync(cmd,args,{cwd:scratch,encoding:"utf8",timeout:120000,maxBuffer:4<<20,...options});
    assert.equal(r.status,0,(r.stdout??"")+(r.stderr??"")); return r.stdout.trim();
  };
  // The source stays at its commissioned version. An isolated 0.4.36 package
  // alias forces replacement even when the candidate and old CLI are 0.4.35.
  // The native executable bytes are unchanged, and this is never published.
  const candidate="0.4.36";
  for(const [i,path] of [client,platform].entries()) {
    const unpack=join(scratch,"candidate-"+i);mkdirSync(unpack);
    sync("tar",["-xzf",resolve(path),"-C",unpack]);
    const pkg=join(unpack,"package"),manifest=JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(pkg,"package.json")));
    manifest.version=candidate;
    if(manifest.name==="snapback4") for(const name of Object.keys(manifest.optionalDependencies??{})) if(name.startsWith("snapback4-")) manifest.optionalDependencies[name]=candidate;
    writeFileSync(join(pkg,"package.json"),JSON.stringify(manifest));
    const packed=JSON.parse(sync("npm",["pack","--ignore-scripts","--json","--pack-destination",unpack],{cwd:pkg}))[0];
    const bytes=readFileSync(join(unpack,packed.filename));
    artifacts.push({manifest,bytes,integrity:"sha512-"+createHash("sha512").update(bytes).digest("base64")});
  }
  const oldRegistries={};
  // 0.4.35 may still be in its publication gate; keep source provenance explicit.
  for(const version of ["0.4.33","0.4.35"]) {
    let response=await fetch(`https://registry.npmjs.org/snapback4/${version}`,{signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000)});
    let registry="https://registry.npmjs.org";
    if(response.status===404 && version==="0.4.35") {
      registry="http://127.0.0.1:4873";
      response=await fetch(`${registry}/snapback4/${version}`,{signal:AbortSignal.timeout(10000)});
    }
    assert.equal(response.status,200,`old ${version} must be available from its recorded registry`);
    const metadata=await response.json();oldRegistries[version]={registry,integrity:metadata.dist.integrity};
  }
  let origin;
  registry=createServer(async(req,res)=>{
    try {
      const path=decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url,"http://localhost").pathname);
      const item=artifacts.find(a=>path==="/"+a.manifest.name||path==="/"+a.manifest.name+"/"+candidate||path==="/"+a.manifest.name+"/latest");
      if(path.startsWith("/_tar/")){const a=artifacts[Number(path.slice(6))];if(!a){res.writeHead(404).end();return;}res.end(a.bytes);return;}
      if(item){const manifest={...item.manifest,dist:{tarball:origin+"/_tar/"+artifacts.indexOf(item),integrity:item.integrity}};res.setHeader("content-type","application/json");res.end(JSON.stringify(path==="/"+manifest.name?{name:manifest.name,"dist-tags":{latest:candidate},versions:{[candidate]:manifest}}:manifest));return;}
      const upstream=await fetch("https://registry.npmjs.org"+req.url,{signal:AbortSignal.timeout(30000)});
      res.writeHead(upstream.status,{"content-type":upstream.headers.get("content-type")??"application/json"});res.end(Buffer.from(await upstream.arrayBuffer()));
    }catch(error){res.writeHead(502).end(String(error));}
  });
  await new Promise(resolve=>registry.listen(0,"127.0.0.1",resolve));origin="http://127.0.0.1:"+registry.address().port;
  const env={...process.env,CI:"1",npm_config_registry:origin,npm_config_audit:"false",npm_config_fund:"false",npm_config_update_notifier:"false"};
  delete env.SNAPBACK4_BIN;delete env.PORT_LO;delete env.PORT_HI;
  const recorder=join(scratch,"capture.cjs");
  writeFileSync(recorder,`const {spawnSync}=require('node:child_process'); const {writeFileSync}=require('node:fs');
const [command,...args]=process.argv.slice(2); const result=spawnSync(command,args,{encoding:'utf8',timeout:170000,maxBuffer:8<<20});
writeFileSync(process.env.DX5_CAPTURE_RESULT,JSON.stringify({stdout:result.stdout??'',stderr:result.stderr??'',status:result.status}));
if(result.status!==0) {process.stdout.write(result.stdout??'');process.stderr.write(result.stderr??'');} process.exit(result.status??1);
`);
    for(const version of ["0.4.33","0.4.35"]) for(const flags of [[],["--quiet"],["--json","--quiet"]]) {
      const cwd=join(scratch,version+"-"+(flags.join("-")||"ordinary"));mkdirSync(join(cwd,"snapback"),{recursive:true});mkdirSync(join(cwd,"scripts"));
      const run=async(cmd,args,extra={})=>{
        const capture=join(cwd,"command-result.json");
        await runToolchainCommand(process.execPath,[recorder,cmd,...args],{cwd,env:{...env,...extra,DX5_CAPTURE_RESULT:capture},timeout:180000});
        return JSON.parse(readFileSync(capture));
      };
      writeFileSync(join(cwd,"package.json"),JSON.stringify({name:"old-cli-proof",private:true,type:"module"}));
      await run("npm",["install","--save-exact",`snapback4@${version}`],{npm_config_registry:oldRegistries[version].registry});
      assert.equal(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd,"node_modules/snapback4/package.json"))).version,version);
      const oldWrapper=readFileSync(join(cwd,"node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js"));
      assert.doesNotMatch(oldWrapper.toString(),/firstCheckWarnings/);
      writeFileSync(join(cwd,"snapback/schema.q"),"use identity password\ntable notes:\n  body: text <=80\n  public\nquery list():\n  return notes first 10 by .id\n");
      writeFileSync(join(cwd,"scripts/legacy.mjs"),'startDev(); fetch("/seed"); const rows=JSON.parse(run(["data","notes","--json"])); rows.map(row=>row.id);\n');
      const upgrade=await run("npx",["--no-install","snapback4","upgrade",candidate,...flags]);
      assert.equal(JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(cwd,"node_modules/snapback4/package.json"))).version,candidate);
      assert.match(readFileSync(join(cwd,"node_modules/snapback4/bin/snapback4.js"),"utf8"),/firstCheckWarnings/);
      const transcript=upgrade.stdout+upgrade.stderr;
      if(!flags.includes("--quiet")) for(const phrase of [/doors are CLOSED/,/startDev\(\) persists/,/JSON.parse\(stdout\).rows/,/scripts\/legacy.mjs/]) assert.match(transcript,phrase);
      if(flags.includes("--json")) assert.equal(JSON.parse(upgrade.stdout).ok,true);
      const check=await run("npx",["--no-install","snapback4","check","--json"]);
      assert.equal(JSON.parse(check.stdout).ok,true);
      for(const phrase of [/doors are CLOSED/,/startDev\(\) persists/,/JSON.parse\(stdout\).rows/,/scripts\/legacy.mjs/]) assert.match(check.stderr,phrase,"captured old quiet output must not acknowledge warnings");
      results.push({from:version,flags,candidate,oldPackage:oldRegistries[version],oldWrapperSha256:createHash("sha256").update(oldWrapper).digest("hex"),status:"passed",acquisition:"installed old npx snapback4 upgrade",warnings:flags.includes("--quiet")?"deferred to next check":"during upgrade and next visible check"});
    }
    return results;
  } finally {if(registry?.listening){registry.closeAllConnections();await new Promise(resolve=>registry.close(resolve));}rmSync(scratch,{recursive:true,force:true});}
}
