//! Query result paths and nullable reads of private side tables.
use super::*;

pub(super) const VIEWER_KEY: &str = "$lookupViewerKey";
const TARGET_ROW: &str = "$lookupTargetRow";

// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#151-evidence-and-decision — compatibility keeps
// fallthrough null; check must name a concrete uncovered source path.
pub(super) fn fallthrough(decl: &ast::ProgramDecl) -> Diagnostic {
    fn path(body: &[S], implicit: bool) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
        let mut route = Vec::new();
        for (at, stmt) in body.iter().enumerate() {
            match stmt {
                S::Return { .. } => return None,
                S::Expr { .. } if implicit && at + 1 == body.len() => return None,
                S::Require { condition: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(false), .. }, .. } => return None,
                S::If { then_body, else_body, site, .. } => {
                    let completes = implicit && at + 1 == body.len();
                    let (arm, rest) = if let Some(rest) = path(then_body, completes) { ("true", rest) }
                        else if let Some(rest) = path(else_body, completes) { ("false", rest) }
                        else { return None };
                    route.push(format!("if at {site} is {arm}")); route.extend(rest);
                }
                S::For { site, .. } => route.push(format!("loop at {site} has zero iterations")),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
        Some(route)
    }
    let route = path(&decl.body, true).unwrap_or_default();
    let route = if route.is_empty() { "entry → end".into() } else { format!("{} → end", route.join(" → ")) };
    Diagnostic::new(&decl.site, "N_QUERY_FALLTHROUGH",
        format!("query {} can finish without a result ({route}); that path returns null", decl.name),
        "return a value on this path: add `return []` for an intentionally empty list, or `return null` for intentional absence; a return inside a possibly empty loop does not cover its zero-iteration path").guide("programs")
}

impl Binder<'_> {
    fn lookup_rule_proved(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
        let mut copy = value.clone();
        let mut target = false;
        super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy, &mut |node| {
            if matches!(node, A::Name { name, .. } if name == TARGET_ROW) { target = true; }
        });
        if !target { return self.point_guard_safe(value) && self.authorization_proved(value, true); }
        match value {
            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "or" => self.lookup_rule_proved(left) || self.lookup_rule_proved(right),
            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" => self.lookup_rule_proved(left) && self.lookup_rule_proved(right),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#152-lookup-contract-and-diagnostics — transport
    // only source advice facts across the helper's captured argument boundary.
    pub(super) fn helper_lookup_points(&self, function: &ast::FunctionDecl, args: &[A]) -> Vec<ReadExpr> {
        fn closed(value: &A, parameters: &[ast::ArgDecl]) -> bool {
            let mut value = value.clone();
            let mut safe = true;
            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut value, &mut |node| match node {
                A::Name { name, .. } if name != "viewer" && name != "now" && !parameters.iter().any(|p| p.name == *name) => safe = false,
                A::Focal { .. } | A::Write { .. } | A::Call { .. } => safe = false,
                _ => {}
            });
            safe
        }
        fn substitute(value: &mut A, parameters: &[ast::ArgDecl], args: &[A]) {
            super::super::intent_walk::expression(value, &mut |node| {
                if let A::Name { name, .. } = node && let Some(at) = parameters.iter().position(|p| p.name == *name)
                    && let Some(argument) = args.get(at) { *node = argument.clone(); }
            });
        }
        fn collect(catalog: &Catalog, function: &ast::FunctionDecl, args: &[A], stack: &mut Vec<String>, fuel: &mut usize, reads: &mut Vec<ReadExpr>) {
            if stack.len() >= 32 || stack.contains(&function.name) { return; }
            stack.push(function.name.clone());
            let mut body = match &function.body { ast::FunctionBody::Block(body) => body.clone(), ast::FunctionBody::Expression(value) => vec![S::Return { value: value.clone(), site: value.site().clone() }] };
            super::super::intent_walk::statements(&mut body, &mut |node| {
                if *fuel == 0 { return; } *fuel -= 1;
                match node {
                    A::Read(read) if read.keys.iter().all(|key| closed(&key.value, &function.args)) => {
                        let mut value = A::Read(read.clone()); substitute(&mut value, &function.args, args);
                        if let A::Read(read) = value { reads.push(read); }
                    }
                    A::Call { name, args: arguments, .. } if arguments.iter().all(|value| closed(value, &function.args)) => {
                        if let Some(callee) = catalog.functions.get(name) {
                            let mut arguments = arguments.clone();
                            for value in &mut arguments { substitute(value, &function.args, args); }
                            collect(catalog, callee, &arguments, stack, fuel, reads);
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            });
            stack.pop();
        }
        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Mutation { return Vec::new(); }
        let identity: Vec<_> = function.args.iter().map(|p| A::Name { name: p.name.clone(), site: p.site.clone() }).collect();
        let mut reads = Vec::new();
        collect(self.catalog, function, &identity, &mut Vec::new(), &mut 512, &mut reads);
        reads.into_iter().filter(|read| read.keys.iter().all(|key| closed(&key.value, &function.args))).filter(|read| {
            let mut value = A::Read(read.clone()); substitute(&mut value, &function.args, args);
            let A::Read(actual) = value else { return false };
            self.point_guard_safe(&A::Read(actual.clone())) && (self.point_present(&actual) || self.lookup_point_present(&actual) || self.row_independent_read_proved(&actual.table))
        }).collect()
    }
    // This marker suppresses advice only; it cannot authorize a runtime read
    // or enter the binder's authority/presence proof domains.
    pub(super) fn lookup_viewer_key(&self, value: &A) -> bool {
        if matches!(value, A::Name { name, .. } if name == "viewer" || self.binding(name)
            .is_some_and(|binding| binding.fields.contains_key(&vec![VIEWER_KEY.into()]))) { return true; }
        let equal = A::Binary { op: "=".into(), left: Box::new(value.clone()), right: Box::new(A::Name { name: "viewer".into(), site: value.site().clone() }), site: value.site().clone() };
        self.point_guard_safe(value) && self.authorization_proved(&equal, true)
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — asserted and same-table reads still need reader authority; blind writes do not pass here.
    pub(super) fn optional_lookup_note(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, point: &Read) {
        if self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Mutation || self.point_present(read) || self.lookup_point_present(read)
            || self.row_independent_read_proved(&read.table) { return; }
        let table = &self.schema.tables[&read.table];
        if table.ephemeral.is_some() || self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some() { return; }
        let index = &table.indexes[&point.index];
        let pinned = index.components.iter().zip(&point.key).filter_map(|(part, value)| part.column().map(|name| (name, value))).collect();
        if snapback4_core::engine::rules::prefix_visibility(&read.table, table, &table.rules.read, &pinned).decided_true { return; }
        if let Some(rule) = self.catalog.tables[&read.table].decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read) {
            let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
            let keys: BTreeMap<_, _> = read.keys.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(at, key)|
                Some((if named { key.column.clone()? } else { index.components.get(at)?.column()?.clone() },
                    if self.lookup_viewer_key(&key.value) { A::Name { name: "viewer".into(), site: key.site.clone() } } else { key.value.clone() }))).collect();
            let mut condition = rule.value.clone();
            // Rule constants and target fields occupy their own namespaces.
            // Freeze/seal them before injecting lexical caller key expressions.
            maintain_types::normalize_rule_constants(&mut condition);
            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition, &mut |value| {
                if let A::Member { base, .. } = value && let A::Focal { site } = base.as_ref() {
                    **base = A::Name { name: TARGET_ROW.into(), site: site.clone() };
                }
            });
            super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition, &mut |value| {
                if let A::Member { base, name, .. } = value && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Name { name, .. } if name == TARGET_ROW)
                    && let Some(key) = keys.get(name) { *value = key.clone(); }
            });
            if self.lookup_rule_proved(&condition) { return; }
        }
        let rule = table.through.as_ref().map_or_else(|| format!("read <- {}", table.rules.read.quarry()), |through| format!("read through .{}", through.reference));
        let location = self.catalog.tables[&read.table].decl.rules.iter().find(|rule| rule.effect == ast::Effect::Read)
            .map(|rule| rule.site.to_string()).unwrap_or_else(|| self.catalog.tables[&read.table].decl.site.to_string());
        if self.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" && note.site == read.site) { return; }
        self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(&read.site, "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED",
            format!("mutation {}: lookup of {} uses `{rule}` ({location}); its key and preceding guards do not prove read authority. A hidden, expired or missing row returns null; `! else CODE` refuses with that authored code for all three. A later field/default or require may report a different business condition", self.name, read.table),
            "review whether these callers should read this data; use a deliberately readable projection containing only the needed fields, or keep null as intentional absence. An id and a later write rule do not grant read authority; do not widen private rows just to read one field").guide("rules"));
    }
}
//! Advisory mismatches between a program's caller gate and its table rule.
use super::catalog::Catalog;
use crate::{ast::*, Diagnostic};
use snapback4_core::schema::{Schema, RuleExpr, RuleTerm};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
fn seat(expr: &Expr) -> Option<&str> {
    let Expr::Binary { op,left,right,.. }=expr else { return None; };
    if op == "and" { return seat(left).or_else(|| seat(right)); }
    if op != "=" { return None; }
    let name = |e: &Expr| matches!(e, Expr::Name { name,.. } if name == "viewer");
    let value = if name(left) { right.as_ref() } else if name(right) { left.as_ref() } else { return None; };
    match value { Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(s),.. } if s.starts_with("service:") => Some(s), _=>None }
}
fn fields(rule: &RuleExpr) -> Vec<String> {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Field(f))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Field(f),RuleTerm::Viewer)=>vec![f.clone()],
        RuleExpr::And(rs)|RuleExpr::Or(rs)=>rs.iter().flat_map(fields).collect(),
        _=>vec![]
    }
}
fn required_owners(rule:&RuleExpr)->Vec<String> {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::And(parts)=>parts.iter().flat_map(required_owners).collect(),
        RuleExpr::Or(parts)=>{
            let Some(first)=parts.first() else {return vec![]};
            let mut common=required_owners(first);
            for part in &parts[1..] {let have=required_owners(part);common.retain(|f|have.contains(f));}
            common
        }
        other=>fields(other),
    }
}
fn admits_seat(rule: &RuleExpr, seat: &str) -> bool {
    match rule {
        RuleExpr::Allow|RuleExpr::Public { .. }=>true,
        RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Viewer,RuleTerm::Literal(v))|RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::Literal(v),RuleTerm::Viewer)=>v.as_str().ok()==Some(seat),
        RuleExpr::Or(rs)=>rs.iter().any(|r|admits_seat(r,seat)),
        _=>false
    }
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.002#7 — these notes state conditional argument requirements, never an impossibility theorem over dynamic inputs.
pub(super) fn notes(catalog: &Catalog, schema: &Schema) -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for doc in &catalog.documents {
        for table in &doc.tables {
            for column in &table.columns {
                if column.type_.kind==TypeKind::Principal && let Some(Expr::Literal {value:Literal::Str(value),..})=&column.default
                    && catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.personas).any(|p|p==value) {
                    out.push(Diagnostic::new(&column.site,"N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL",format!("{}.{} defaults to bare persona '{value}', whose dev principal is 'dev:{value}'",table.name,column.name),format!("use `default 'dev:{value}'` for development, or supply the authenticated principal in the mutation for production")).guide("personas"));
                }
            }
        }
        for program in &doc.programs {
            if program.native { continue; }
            body(catalog,schema,program,&program.body,None,&mut out);
        }
    }
    out
}
fn body(catalog:&Catalog,schema:&Schema,program:&ProgramDecl,statements:&[Stmt], mut gate:Option<String>,out:&mut Vec<Diagnostic>) {
    let mut lookups: BTreeMap<String,(String,Vec<String>)>=BTreeMap::new();
    for statement in statements {
        if let Stmt::Require { condition,code,site,.. }=statement {
            if program.kind==ProgramKind::Mutation && let Some(seat)=seat(condition) { gate=Some(seat.into()); }
            for (alias,(table,owners)) in &lookups {
                let mut uses_owner=false; let mut copy=condition.clone();
                super::intent_walk::expression(&mut copy,&mut |e| if let Expr::Member { base,name,.. }=e && matches!(base.as_ref(),Expr::Name { name,.. } if name==alias) && owners.contains(name) { uses_owner=true; });
                if uses_owner && code != "NOT_FOUND" {
                    out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN",format!("{}: callers who cannot read {table} get NOT_FOUND at `{alias}`'s lookup, never {code} from this require",program.name),
                        format!("put a caller authorization check before the lookup if {code} must win; otherwise keep NOT_FOUND as the privacy-preserving answer and test it (guide rules)")).guide("rules"));
                }
            }
        }
        if let Stmt::Bind {names,..}=statement {for name in names {lookups.remove(name);}}
        if program.kind!=ProgramKind::Job && let Stmt::Bind { names,value:Expr::Assert { value,code,.. },.. }=statement && code=="NOT_FOUND" && let Expr::Read(read)=value.as_ref() {
            if let [name]=names.as_slice() && let Some(table)=schema.table(&read.table) {
                let owners=required_owners(&table.rules.read);
                if !owners.is_empty() { lookups.insert(name.clone(),(read.table.clone(),owners)); }
            }
        }
        let mut copy=vec![statement.clone()];
        // Inspect only this statement's expressions; nested control flow receives its own gate below.
        if !matches!(statement,Stmt::If { .. }|Stmt::For { .. }|Stmt::As { .. }) {
            super::intent_walk::statements(&mut copy,&mut |expr| {
                let Expr::Write { op,table,fields:payload,site,.. }=expr else { return; };
                let Some(def)=schema.table(table) else { return; };
                for field in payload.iter() {
                    let principal=catalog.tables.get(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(&field.name)).is_some_and(|t|t.kind==TypeKind::Principal);
                    if principal && let Some(Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(value),.. })=&field.value
                        && catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d|&d.personas).any(|p| p==value) {
                        out.push(Diagnostic::new(&field.site,"N_PERSONA_PRINCIPAL",format!("{table}.{} stores bare persona '{value}', but its dev viewer is 'dev:{value}'",field.name),format!("write 'dev:{value}' for development, or an authenticated account principal in production; personas are names, not principals")).guide("personas"));
                    }
                }
                if program.kind!=ProgramKind::Mutation { return; }
                // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — source advice can name a private unique clause; public failures cannot.
                if matches!(op, WriteOp::Insert | WriteOp::Update | WriteOp::Upsert)
                    && (def.through.is_some() || def.not_before.is_some() || !matches!(def.rules.read, RuleExpr::Allow | RuleExpr::Public { .. }))
                    && !(def.through.is_none() && def.not_before.is_none() && gate.as_deref().is_some_and(|seat| admits_seat(&def.rules.read, seat)))
                    && let Some(refusals) = schema.unique_refusals.get(table) {
                    for (index_name, refusal) in refusals {
                        let Some(index) = def.index(index_name) else { continue; };
                        if *op == WriteOp::Update && !payload.iter().any(|field| field.spread || index.columns().contains(&field.name)) { continue; }
                        let location = catalog.tables.get(table).and_then(|t| t.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == index_name)).map(|i| &i.site).unwrap_or(site);
                        if out.iter().any(|n| n.code == "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" && n.site == *location) { continue; }
                        let mut read = def.through.as_ref().map_or_else(|| format!("read <- {}", def.rules.read.quarry()), |through| format!("read through .{}", through.reference));
                        if let Some(column) = &def.not_before { read.push_str(&format!("; not before .{column}")); }
                        out.push(Diagnostic::new(location, "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN",
                            format!("{table}'s unique {} declares else {}, but conflicts the caller cannot read under `{read}` use generic E_RULE (or an undisclosed target miss), never that named code. No stored conflict is inferred by this source note", index.columns().join(", "), refusal.code),
                            "keep private-row existence undisclosed; if the product requires a public availability check, model a minimal readable key projection or an operator-mediated claim. Test readable conflicts separately; do not widen private rows to obtain the named code").guide("rules"));
                    }
                }
                let Some(seat)=gate.as_deref() else { return; };
                let effect=match op { WriteOp::Insert=>"insert",WriteOp::Update=>"update", _=>return };
                let rule=if effect=="insert" { &def.rules.insert } else { &def.rules.update };
                if admits_seat(rule,seat) { return; }
                let others=required_owners(rule).into_iter().filter(|owner| payload.iter().find(|f|&f.name==owner).map_or(effect=="update",|f| {
                    let value=f.value.clone().unwrap_or_else(||Expr::Name { name:f.name.clone(),site:f.site.clone() });
                    !matches!(value,Expr::Name { ref name,.. } if name=="viewer") && !matches!(value,Expr::Literal { value:Literal::Str(ref s),.. } if s==seat)
                })).collect::<Vec<_>>();
                if others.is_empty() { return; }
                out.push(Diagnostic::new(site,"N_WRITE_DENIED",format!("table authorization layer: {} admits only '{seat}', but {table}'s {effect} rule `{}` requires the written {} to be this caller; writing another principal's value refuses E_RULE",program.name,rule.quarry(),others.join(", ")),
                    format!("if this trusted seat should write other principals' rows, amend `{effect} <- {}` to `{effect} <- ({}) or viewer = '{seat}'`; the mutation require alone grants no table authority (guide deploy, first admin); see: guide rule-recipes role-workflow",rule.quarry(),rule.quarry())).guide("rules"));
            });
        }
        match statement {
            Stmt::If { condition,then_body,else_body,.. }=> { body(catalog,schema,program,then_body,seat(condition).map(String::from).or_else(||gate.clone()),out); body(catalog,schema,program,else_body,gate.clone(),out); }
            Stmt::For { body:inner,.. }=>body(catalog,schema,program,inner,gate.clone(),out),
            Stmt::As { body:inner,.. }=>body(catalog,schema,program,inner,None,out),
            Stmt::Return { .. }|Stmt::Break { .. }|Stmt::Continue { .. }=>break,
            _=>{}
        }
    }
}

use snapback4_lang::compile;
use snapback4_core::{Context, Engine, Value, value::object};

const TABLES: &str = "table funding:
  owner: principal
  limit: int
  expires: time ?
  unique owner
  read <- .owner = viewer
  expire at .expires
  online only
table work:
  n: int
  public
  update <- allow
  online only
";

fn checked(body: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Bound {
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), format!("{TABLES}{body}"))]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    bound
}

#[test]
fn optional_private_side_reads_name_the_hiding_rule_and_null_consequence() {
    for body in [
        "mutation required(who: principal):\n  return funding[who] ! else NO\n",
        "mutation approve(who: principal):\n  row = funding[who]\n  require (row.limit ?? 0) > 1 else NEEDS_APPROVAL\n  return null\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation approve(who: principal):\n  return threshold(who)\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation approve(who: principal, other: principal):\n  x = who\n  require x = viewer else NO\n  x = other\n  return threshold(x)\n",
    ] {
        let bound = checked(body);
        let notes: Vec<_> = bound.notes.iter().filter(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED").collect();
        assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1, "{:?}", bound.notes);
        assert!(notes[0].message.contains("read <- .owner = viewer") && notes[0].message.contains("returns null"), "{}", notes[0]);
        assert_eq!(notes[0].site.file, "schema.q");
        assert!(notes[0].site.line > 0);
        assert!(notes[0].rewrite.contains("projection"));
    }
}

#[test]
fn required_same_table_lookup_explains_its_read_gate_but_blind_writes_do_not() {
    let bound = checked("mutation patch(who: principal):\n  row = funding[who] ! else NO_ROW\n  update funding[row] {limit: 1}\n  return null\nmutation blind(who: principal):\n  update funding[owner = who] {limit: 1}\n  return null\n");
    let notes: Vec<_> = bound.notes.iter().filter(|n| n.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED").collect();
    assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1, "{:?}", bound.notes);
    assert!(notes[0].message.contains("mutation patch") && notes[0].message.contains("read <- .owner = viewer"));
    assert!(notes[0].message.contains("! else CODE") && notes[0].rewrite.contains("do not widen private rows"));
}

#[test]
fn named_unique_advice_is_source_only_and_does_not_warn_on_public_keys() {
    for read in ["read <- .owner = viewer", "public"] {
        let source = format!("table claims:\n  key: text 1..80\n  owner: principal\n  unique key else TAKEN\n  {read}\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\nmutation add(key: text 1..80):\n  return insert claims {{key, owner:viewer}}\n");
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let notes: Vec<_> = bound.notes.iter().filter(|n| n.code == "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN").collect();
        if read == "public" { assert!(notes.is_empty()); }
        else {
            assert_eq!(notes.len(), 1);
            assert_eq!(notes[0].site.line, 4);
            assert!(notes[0].message.contains("else TAKEN") && notes[0].message.contains("generic E_RULE"));
            assert!(notes[0].rewrite.contains("operator-mediated claim"));
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn otherwise_readable_expired_rows_are_absent_in_mutations_and_helper_assertions() {
    let bound = checked("fn need(who: principal): funding[who] ! else NOT_FOUND\nmutation required(who: principal):\n  row = need(who)\n  require row.limit > 1 else TOO_LOW\n  return row.limit\nmutation early(who: principal):\n  require false else FIRST\n  return need(who)\nmutation optional(who: principal):\n  return funding[who]\n");
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([("funding".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("expired".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("viewer".into())), ("limit", Value::Int(3)), ("expires", Value::Int(10))]))]).unwrap();
    let ctx = |now| Context::new("viewer", [("who".into(), Value::Id("viewer".into()))].into()).at(now);
    assert!(matches!(engine.mutate("required", None, ctx(1)).unwrap().state, snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { .. }));
    let optional = engine.mutate("optional", None, ctx(20)).unwrap();
    assert!(matches!(optional.state, snapback4_core::WriteState::Sent { result: Value::Null, .. }));
    for (op, code) in [("required", "NOT_FOUND"), ("early", "FIRST")] {
        let result = engine.mutate(op, None, ctx(20)).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(result.state, snapback4_core::WriteState::Failed { ref why, .. } if why.code == code), "{result:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn exact_read_authority_guards_assertions_and_blind_writes_stay_quiet() {
    for body in [
        "mutation mine():\n  return funding[viewer]\n",
        "mutation guarded(who: principal):\n  require who = viewer else NO\n  return funding[who]\n",
        "mutation guarded(who: principal):\n  require exists funding[who] else NO\n  return funding[who]\n",
        "mutation blind(w: work):\n  update work[w] { n: 1 }\n",
        "query optional(who: principal):\n  return funding[who]\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation guarded(who: principal):\n  require who = viewer else NO\n  return threshold(who)\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation guarded():\n  return threshold(viewer)\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation guarded(who: principal):\n  require exists funding[who] else NO\n  return threshold(who)\n",
        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nfn outer(who: principal): threshold(who)\nmutation guarded(who: principal):\n  require exists funding[who] else NO\n  return outer(who)\n",
    ] {
        let bound = checked(body);
        assert!(!bound.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"), "{body}: {:?}", bound.notes);
    }
}

#[test]
fn inherited_read_authority_is_named_without_exposing_capture_fields() {
    let source = "table parents:\n  owner: principal\n  immutable owner\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  online only\ntable funding:\n  parent: parents\n  limit: int\n  immutable parent\n  read through .parent\n  online only\nmutation threshold(id: funding):\n  row = funding[id]\n  require (row.limit ?? 0) > 1 else NEEDS_APPROVAL\n  return null\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|n| n.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED").unwrap();
    assert!(note.message.contains("read through .parent"), "{note}");
    assert!(!note.message.contains("$"), "{note}");
}

        "fn threshold(who: principal): funding[who]\nmutation guarded(who: principal, other: principal):\n  x = who\n  require exists funding[x] else NO\n  x = other\n  return threshold(x)\n",

    }

#[test]
fn target_rule_constants_and_unpinned_fields_cannot_capture_caller_namespaces() {
    let source = format!("{}mutation approve(deny: bool):\n  require deny else STOP\n  return funding[viewer]\n", TABLES.replace("read <- .owner = viewer", "read <- .owner = viewer and deny"));
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(bound.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"));

    let source = format!("{}mutation approve(w: work, f: funding):\n  return work[w] {{ limit: funding[f].limit if .owner = viewer else null }}\n", TABLES.replace("table work:\n  n: int", "table work:\n  owner: principal\n  n: int"));
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(bound.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"));

    let bound = checked("mutation guarded(deny: principal):\n  require deny = viewer else NO\n  return funding[deny]\n");
    assert!(!bound.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED"));
}

#[test]
fn optional_and_required_lookups_keep_hidden_absent_and_expired_equivalent() {
    let bound = checked("fn peek(who: principal): funding[who]\nquery optional(who: principal):\n  return peek(who)\nquery required(who: principal):\n  row = funding[who] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require row.limit > 1 else TOO_LOW\n  return row.limit\n");
    let mut engine = Engine::memory(bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program)).unwrap();
    engine.seed([
        ("funding".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("a".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("hidden".into())), ("limit", Value::Int(3)), ("expires", Value::Null)])),
        ("funding".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("b".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("expiry".into())), ("limit", Value::Int(3)), ("expires", Value::Int(10))])),
        ("funding".into(), object([("id", Value::Id("c".into())), ("owner", Value::Id("viewer".into())), ("limit", Value::Int(3)), ("expires", Value::Null)])),
    ]).unwrap();
    let mut refusals = Vec::new();
    let mut meters = Vec::new();
    for who in ["hidden", "absent", "expiry"] {
        let viewer = if who == "expiry" { "expiry" } else { "viewer" };
        let ctx = || Context::new(viewer, [("who".into(), Value::Id(who.into()))].into()).at(20);
        let optional = engine.query("optional", ctx()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(optional.data, Value::Null);
        assert!(optional.complete);
        meters.push(optional.metered);
        refusals.push(engine.query("required", ctx()).unwrap_err());
    }
    assert!(refusals.iter().all(|refusal| refusal.code == "NOT_FOUND"));
    assert!(meters.iter().all(|meter| *meter == meters[0]), "{meters:?}");
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&refusals[0]).unwrap(), serde_json::to_value(&refusals[1]).unwrap());
    assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&refusals[0]).unwrap(), serde_json::to_value(&refusals[2]).unwrap());
    let visible = engine.query("required", Context::new("viewer", [("who".into(), Value::Id("viewer".into()))].into()).at(20)).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(visible.data, Value::Int(3));
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#8-wave-18-developer-loop-amendment-dx4 — bounded candidate scan, never an automatic script rewrite.
// Project-specific migration advice. Read authored files only; never print their contents.
import { readdirSync, readFileSync, lstatSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync, renameSync, rmSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
const excluded = new Set(['node_modules', '.git', '.snapback4', 'generated', 'dist', 'build', 'target', 'deploy', 'logs', 'store', 'output', '.runtime']);
export function upgradeWarnings(root, declarations) {
  const doors = [], persistence = [], dataJson = [];
  let scanned = 0, incomplete = false;
  const walk = (dir, prefix = '', depth = 0) => {
    if (scanned > 10000 || depth > 8) { incomplete = true; return; }
    let entries;
    try { entries = readdirSync(dir, {withFileTypes: true}); } catch { incomplete = true; return; }
    for (const entry of entries.sort((a,b)=>a.name.localeCompare(b.name))) {
      if (++scanned > 10000) { incomplete = true; return; }
      if (excluded.has(entry.name) || entry.name.startsWith('.')) continue;
      const relative = prefix + entry.name, path = join(dir, entry.name);
      if (entry.isDirectory()) { walk(path, relative + '/', depth + 1); continue; }
      if (!entry.isFile() || !/\.(?:[cm]?[jt]sx?|sh|json)$/.test(entry.name)) continue;
      let size; try { size = lstatSync(path).size; } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      if (size > 1048576) { incomplete = true; continue; }
      let source; try { source = readFileSync(path, 'utf8'); } catch { incomplete = true; continue; }
      if (/\/seed\b|\/dev\/|x-snapback-persona/.test(source)) doors.push(relative);
      if (/\bstartDev\s*\(/.test(source)) persistence.push(relative);
      // Candidate scan: shell commands, argv arrays and npm-script strings.
      // Never infer how the parsed result is used or rewrite application code.
      const commandText = source.replace(/\s+/g, ' ');
      if (/(?:\bsnapback4(?:@[^\s"']+)?\s+data\b|["']data(?:["']|\s))[^;]{0,2000}--json/.test(commandText)
        || /(?:\bsnapback4\b[^;]{0,2000}--json[^;]{0,2000}\bdata\b|["']--json["'][^;]{0,2000}["']data["'])/.test(commandText)) dataJson.push(relative);
    }
  };
  walk(root);
  const warnings = [];
  const passwordOnly = /^\s*use[ \t]+identity[ \t]+password\b/m.test(declarations) && !/^\s*use[ \t]+identity(?:[ \t]*(?:--[^\n]*)?)?$/m.test(declarations);
  if (passwordOnly) warnings.push('password-only dev doors are CLOSED since 0.4.33: /seed, /dev/* and persona headers refuse. Use deploy staging --staging plus serve staging for real users (guide staging); local fixtures explicitly opt into dev --unsafe-dev-doors.' + (doors.length ? ' Review: ' + doors.join(', ') : ' No matching dev-door scripts found.'));
  if (persistence.length) warnings.push('startDev() persists since 0.4.33: reruns see old rows and share the dev store. For disposable acceptance use startDev({args:["--memory"]}); keep persistence for continuity. Review (check each call for explicit memory): ' + persistence.join(', '));
  if (dataJson.length) warnings.push('data --json returns {rows, more, limit} since 0.4.35, replacing the bare array. One-line fix: JSON.parse(stdout).rows (was JSON.parse(stdout)); use more/limit to detect truncation. Review candidate consumers: ' + dataJson.join(', '));
  if (incomplete) warnings.push('migration scan incomplete (unreadable/large files or depth/file limit); inspect remaining authored scripts for /seed, /dev/*, persona headers, startDev() and data --json array consumers.');
  return warnings;
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#10-wave-20-loop-repairs-dx5 — the OLD CLI invokes the NEW wrapper for its post-upgrade check.
export function firstCheckWarnings(cwd = process.cwd()) {
  if (process.env.SNAPBACK4_UPGRADE_BASELINE === '1') return;
  let temporary;
  try {
    let root = cwd;
    while (!existsSync(join(root, 'package.json'))) { const parent = dirname(root); if (parent === root) return; root = parent; }
    const schema = join(root, 'snapback');
    if (!existsSync(schema)) return;
    const version = JSON.parse(readFileSync(fileURLToPath(new URL('../package.json', import.meta.url)), 'utf8')).version;
    const receipt = join(root, '.snapback4/project-warnings-version.json');
    try { if (JSON.parse(readFileSync(receipt, 'utf8')).version === version) return; } catch { /* First check or unreadable receipt: deliver. */ }
    const declarations = readdirSync(schema).filter(name => name.endsWith('.q')).map(name => readFileSync(join(schema, name), 'utf8')).join('\n');
    const warnings = upgradeWarnings(root, declarations);
    const name = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, 'package.json'), 'utf8')).name ?? root;
    for (const warning of warnings) process.stderr.write(`project ${name}: upgrade warning: ${warning}\n`);
    // Old upgrade --quiet captures and discards check stderr. A pipe is not
    // evidence of delivery: leave advice pending for the next visible check.
    if (warnings.length && !process.stderr.isTTY) return;
    mkdirSync(dirname(receipt), {recursive:true});
    temporary = `${receipt}.${randomUUID()}.tmp`;
    writeFileSync(temporary, JSON.stringify({version}) + '\n', {flag:'wx'});
    renameSync(temporary, receipt);
  } catch {
    process.stderr.write('snapback4: project migration advice unavailable or not acknowledged; check will continue. Review guide changes and run upgrade to scan authored scripts.\n');
  } finally { if (temporary) try { rmSync(temporary, {force:true}); } catch { /* Advisory only. */ } }
}

//! Advice is evidence about known template bytes, or explicitly a heuristic.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — custom proofs own their arguments and assertions.
use std::path::Path;
use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
use serde::Deserialize;

#[derive(Deserialize)]
struct Template { file: String, version: String, template: Option<String>, web: bool, sha256: String }
fn templates() -> &'static [Template] {
    static TEMPLATES: std::sync::OnceLock<Vec<Template>> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    TEMPLATES.get_or_init(|| serde_json::from_str(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).expect("shipped proof digests"))
}
pub(super) fn digest(text: &str) -> String { format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(text.as_bytes())) }
pub(super) fn known_day2(text: &str) -> bool {
    let hash = digest(text);
    templates().iter().any(|t| t.file == "day2" && t.sha256 == hash)
}
/// Exact shipped helper bytes identify its identity mode and starter revision.
pub(super) fn native_template(text: &str) -> Option<(&'static str, bool)> {
    let hash = digest(text);
    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "native" && t.sha256 == hash)
        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref() == Some("password")))
}
pub(super) fn frontend_template(dir: &Path, web: bool) -> Option<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>)> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")).ok()?;
    let hash = digest(&text);
    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "frontend" && t.web == web && t.sha256 == hash)
        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref()))
}
pub(super) fn known_frontend(dir: &Path) -> bool { [false,true].into_iter().any(|web|frontend_template(dir,web).is_some()) }
fn package(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}
pub(super) fn referenced(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> bool {
    package(dir)["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))))
}
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#15-wave-15-starter-advice-day5-2026-10-18 — compare the complete directory capture; no distinguished schema.q is required.
pub(super) fn legacy_differences(dir: &Path, plan: &[(&str, String)]) -> Vec<String> {
    ["v1", "v2"].into_iter().filter_map(|version| {
        let flat = format!("versions/{version}.q");
        let prefix = format!("versions/{version}/");
        let legacy = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(&flat)).ok()?;
        let mut saved = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
        if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join(&prefix)) {
            for entry in entries.flatten() {
                let path = entry.path();
                if path.extension().is_some_and(|ext|ext=="q") && let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                    saved.insert(format!("{prefix}{}",entry.file_name().to_string_lossy()),text);
                }
            }
        }
        for (name,text) in plan.iter().filter(|(name,_)|name.starts_with(&prefix) && name.ends_with(".q")) {
            saved.insert((*name).to_owned(),text.clone());
        }
        if saved.is_empty() || saved.len()==1 && saved.values().next()==Some(&legacy) {return None;}
        let paths=saved.keys().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        Some(format!("info: {flat} differs from {paths}; saved {version} captures snapback/*.q, not the legacy flat source; both remain unchanged evidence. Review the pair before day2:proof"))
    }).collect()
}
pub(super) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    let mut advice = legacy_differences(dir, &[]);
    if let Ok(entries) = std::fs::read_dir(dir.join("versions")) {
        let mut paths = Vec::new();
        for entry in entries.flatten() {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_dir() {
                if let Ok(files) = std::fs::read_dir(&path) { paths.extend(files.flatten().map(|entry|entry.path()).filter(|path|path.extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="q"))); }
            } else if path.extension().is_some_and(|e|e=="q") { paths.push(path); }
        }
        paths.sort();
        for path in paths {
            if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {
                let hash = digest(&text);
                if let Some(t) = templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "todo-schema" && t.sha256 == hash) {
                    advice.push(format!("info: {} is the unmodified {} todo snapshot (sha256 {hash}); replace it with this app's saved schema and contemporary fixtures before day2:proof", path.strip_prefix(dir).unwrap_or(&path).display(), t.version));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/launch.mjs"] {
        if !dir.join(path).exists() && referenced(dir, path) {
            advice.push(format!("info: {path} is missing but package.json still references it; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`, then `snapback4 init --adapt --apply`"));
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")) {
        let hash=digest(&text);
        if let Some(t)=templates().iter().find(|t| t.file=="day2" && t.sha256==hash) {
            if matches!(t.version.as_str(),"0.4.28"|"0.4.29") {
                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} starter: it asserts no business rows; preview `snapback4 init --adapt` to replace it and capture current sources/fixtures",t.version));
            }
            if text.contains("assert.fail(") {
                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} assert.fail business-assertion placeholder (sha256 {hash}); replace BUSINESS_ASSERTION_BEGIN with this app's persisted-row and refusal assertions", t.version));
            }
            if t.version=="0.4.30" || t.version=="0.4.31" {
                let script=package(dir)["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
                if script=="node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" {
                    advice.push("info: the unmodified day-two starter needs its two saved-source arguments; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`".into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")) {
        // Custom JavaScript can hide builds in arbitrary imports/functions. These
        // checks deliberately make no claim about its behavior or ownership.
        let active=text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let flat=active.replace(['\"','\'',',','[',']','(',')'], " ");
        let words=flat.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let serves_dist=words.windows(2).any(|w|w==["startStatic","dist"]);
        let pkg=package(dir);
        let launch=pkg["scripts"]["frontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let pre=pkg["scripts"]["prefrontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
        let builds=|s:&str|s.contains("build")||s.contains("export");
        if serves_dist && !builds(&active) && !builds(launch) && !builds(pre) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs serves dist with no visible build step (heuristic; imported code may build it); build in the proof or its npm script before startStatic(\"dist\")".into());
        }
        if words.windows(3).any(|w|w==["npx","snapback4","dev"]) {
            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs launches npx snapback4 dev (heuristic); use startDev from snapback4/proof for an owned server and bounded cleanup".into());
        }
    }
    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/proof.mjs", "scripts/acceptance.mjs"] {
        let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join(path)) else { continue; };
        let active = text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
        let npx = active.contains("\"npx\"") || active.contains("'npx'");
        let status_only = active.contains("/auth/config") && (active.contains("status === 200") || active.contains("status == 200") || active.contains("[200, 404]") || active.contains("status === 404"));
        if npx && status_only {
            advice.push(format!("info: {path} spawns npx and accepts readiness HTTP status (heuristic; sha256 {}); readiness is not business evidence. Use snapback4/proof hosts and assert persisted rows plus refusals at each release", digest(&text)));
        }
    }
    advice
}

#[test]
fn readiness_npx_advice_is_heuristic_and_does_not_claim_template_ownership() {
    let root=std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("sb4-day5-proof-advice-{}",std::process::id()));
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("scripts")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(root.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"), "spawn('npx', ['snapback4','dev']); fetch('/auth/config'); if (res.ok || res.status === 404) return;").unwrap();

// For SVG/foreignObject controls, click the actual rendered box, not a text
// descendant whose DOM geometry Playwright may interpret differently.
async function clickSvg(page, control) {
  await control.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
  const box = await control.boundingBox();
  assert.ok(box && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0, "SVG control has no rendered box");
  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
}

    let mut additions: Vec<_> = generated.into_iter().skip(1)
        .filter(|(name, _)| {
            if *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" { return inherited_frontend.is_some() || !dir.join(name).exists() && referenced_after(name); }
            if *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { return day2 && (managed_day2 || !dir.join(name).exists() && (enable_day2 || referenced_day2)); }
            if !dir.join(name).exists() {
                return name.starts_with("versions/") || referenced_after(name);
            }
            false
        }).collect();
    additions.extend(config_changes);
    if package != original { additions.push(("package.json", pretty(&package))); }
    Ok(additions)
}

fn launcher_port(dir: &std::path::Path, name: &str) -> Option<u16> {
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/launch.mjs")).ok()?;
    // Recognize the generated fallback expression, not client application code.
    let marker = if name == "backend" { "low === null ? " } else { ": low === null ? " };
    text.split_once(marker)?.1.split_whitespace().next()?.parse().ok()
}


pub(super) fn apply(dir: &std::path::Path, additions: &[(&str, String)]) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    for (name, body) in additions {
        let path = dir.join(name);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap())?;
        if *name == "package.json" || *name == "vite.config.mts" || *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" || *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { super::replace_file(&path, body)?; }
        else {
            use std::io::Write;
            let mut file = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().write(true).create_new(true).open(&path)?;
            file.write_all(body.as_bytes())?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Environment is evaluated whenever Vite loads the config, never at init time.
pub(super) fn runtime_vite(text: &str, backend: u16, frontend: u16) -> String {
    if text.contains("const proofRange") { return text.to_owned(); }
    let header = format!(r#"const proofRange = [process.env.PORT_LO, process.env.PORT_HI];
if (proofRange.some(v => v !== undefined) && (!proofRange.every(v => v && /^\d+$/.test(v)) || Number(proofRange[0]) < 1 || Number(proofRange[1]) > 65535 || Number(proofRange[0]) >= Number(proofRange[1]))) throw Error("PORT_LO/PORT_HI must provide at least two valid ports");
const backendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {backend} : Number(proofRange[0]);
const frontendPort = proofRange[0] === undefined ? {frontend} : backendPort + 1;
process.env.VITE_SNAPBACK_URL ??= `http://127.0.0.1:${{backendPort}}`;
"#);
    let mut result = text.to_owned();
    // All generated configurations have literal ports; preserve unrelated config.
    for port in [frontend, 5173, 4173] { result = result.replace(&format!("port: {port}"), "port: frontendPort"); }
    format!("{header}{result}")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    #[test]
    fn historical_frontend_templates_keep_assertions_and_gain_their_own_build() {

//! Starter leftovers on the init-first path: `init --web` (or `--expo`) wrote
//! the todo starter, then the project replaced `snapback/schema.q`. The
//! starter's own files that still match the bytes init wrote, but no longer
//! fit the schema, are leftovers: `check` names them and `init --adapt`
//! removes or rewrites them. A file the project changed is never touched.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.2 — leftovers are judged by the starter's original bytes, never by name alone.
use super::starter::{self, Body, Platform};
use super::{OwnSchema, own_schema};
use std::path::Path;

/// One unmodified starter file that no longer fits the project's schema.
pub(crate) struct Leftover {
    pub path: &'static str,
    pub why: String,
    /// The text `init --adapt` writes; `None` removes the file.
    pub rewrite: Option<String>,
}

/// Starter operations its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
const STARTER_OPERATIONS: [&str; 4] = ["list", "add", "toggle", "remove"];

/// Whether a schema still has the starter's `todos` table and the operations
/// its fixtures, cases and `Todos` screen call.
pub(super) fn fits_todos(own: &OwnSchema) -> bool {
    own.tables.iter().any(|table| table == "todos")
        && STARTER_OPERATIONS.iter().all(|op| own.operations.iter().any(|name| name == op))
}

/// What the schema in `dir` leaves of the starter, and the advice that goes
/// with it (a sign-in the project must write by hand). No schema: nothing.
pub(crate) fn leftovers(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>) {
    let (found, advice, _) = plan(dir);
    (found, advice)
}

fn plan(dir: &Path) -> (Vec<Leftover>, Vec<String>, Vec<String>) {
    let Ok(Some(own)) = own_schema(dir) else { return (Vec::new(), Vec::new(), Vec::new()) };
    let mut found = Vec::new();
    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    let mut modified = Vec::new();
    let todos = fits_todos(&own);
    for (path, text) in starter::FIXTURES {
        let current = read(dir, path);
        if current.as_ref().is_some_and(|current| *current != super::without_design_references(text)) { modified.push(path.to_owned()); }
        if !todos && current.is_some_and(|current| current == super::without_design_references(text)) {
            let what = if path.ends_with("todos.jsonl") { "rows for the todo starter's `todos` table" } else { "cases for the todo starter's list/add/toggle/remove" };
            found.push(Leftover { path, why: format!("the unmodified {what}; your snapback/*.q has no todos table with those operations, so `snapback4 test` would refuse it"), rewrite: None });
        }
    }
    for platform in [Platform::Web, Platform::Expo] {
        if !screens(dir, platform, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut advice) {
            for path in [platform.paths().0, platform.paths().1] { if read(dir, path).is_some() { modified.push(path.to_owned()); } }
        }
    }
    if let Some(text) = read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts") {
        let wanted = starter::native_smoke(own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests));
        let recognized = if std::fs::symlink_metadata(dir.join("src/native-smoke.ts")).is_ok_and(|m| m.file_type().is_symlink()) { None } else { super::proof_advice::native_template(&text) };
        if recognized.is_none() { modified.push("src/native-smoke.ts".to_owned()); }
        if recognized.is_some() && !smoke_referenced(dir, &found) {
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why: "the unmodified helper is now supplied by snapback4-expo/native-smoke; the planned screens no longer import this copy".into(), rewrite: None });
        } else if text != wanted && let Some((version, password)) = recognized && smoke_referenced(dir, &found) {
            let old = text != starter::native_smoke(password);
            let why = if old {
                let mode = if password != own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests) { "; refresh also updates its sign-in mode" } else { "" };
                format!("an older starter version ({version}; adapt can refresh it){mode}")
            } else {
                "the unmodified native smoke helper uses a different starter sign-in mode (adapt can refresh it)".into()
            };
            found.push(Leftover { path: "src/native-smoke.ts", why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
        }
    }
    if read(dir, "src/native-smoke.ts").is_some() && !smoke_referenced(dir, &found) && !found.iter().any(|f| f.path == "src/native-smoke.ts" && f.rewrite.is_none()) {
        advice.push("info: src/native-smoke.ts has no source reference; review it before a native smoke proof".into());
    }
    if !todos && let Some(web) = [false, true].into_iter().find(|web| super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, *web).is_some_and(|(_, template)| template.is_none())) {
        found.push(Leftover { path: "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", why: "the unmodified todo proof needs your screen's write assertions; keep a runnable frontend scaffold that explicitly reports the missing scenario".into(), rewrite: Some(super::proof::frontend_scaffold(web)) });
    }
    readme(dir, &own, todos, &mut found, &mut modified);
    app_json(dir, &mut found);
    expo_favicon(dir, &mut found);
    (found, advice, modified)
}

// A custom entry/component/proof can reference the helper too. Generated trees
// and dependency directories are not authored source; do not follow symlinks.
fn smoke_referenced(root: &Path, changes: &[Leftover]) -> bool {
    fn walk(root: &Path, dir: &Path, helper: &Path, changes: &[Leftover]) -> bool {
        let Ok(entries)=std::fs::read_dir(dir) else { return true; };
        for entry in entries {
            let Ok(entry)=entry else {return true;};
            let Ok(kind)=entry.file_type() else {return true;};
            let path=entry.path();
            if kind.is_symlink() {
                if matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(),Some("node_modules"|".git"|".snapback4"|".expo"|"target"|"dist"|"build"|"generated")) {continue;}
                return true; // Unknown source may refer back to the local helper.
            }
            if kind.is_dir() {
                if !matches!(entry.file_name().to_str(),Some("node_modules"|".git"|".snapback4"|".expo"|"target"|"dist"|"build"|"generated")) && walk(root,&path,helper,changes) {return true;}
            } else if path!=helper && !path.to_string_lossy().ends_with(".d.ts")
                && path.extension().and_then(|e|e.to_str()).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,"ts"|"tsx"|"js"|"jsx"|"mjs"|"cjs")) {
                let relative = path.strip_prefix(root).unwrap().to_string_lossy();
                let text = if let Some(change) = changes.iter().find(|f| f.path == relative) {
                    let Some(text) = &change.rewrite else { continue; }; Some(text.clone())
                } else { std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok() };
                if text.is_none_or(|text| {
                    let text = text.replace("snapback4-expo/native-smoke", "");
                    if text.contains("/native-smoke") { return true; }
                    let compact: String = text.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace()).collect();
                    if ["from\"native-smoke\"", "from'native-smoke'", "import\"native-smoke\"", "import'native-smoke'"].iter().any(|s| compact.contains(*s)) { return true; }
                    // A CLI mode named native-smoke is not a module reference.
                    // Unknown dynamic imports/requires keep the local helper.
                    ["import(", "require("].iter().any(|call| compact.match_indices(*call).any(|(at, _)| {
                        let argument = &compact[at + call.len()..];
                        !argument.starts_with(['\'', '"']) || argument.starts_with("'native-smoke'") || argument.starts_with("\"native-smoke\"")
                    }))
                }) {return true;}
            }
        }
        false
    }
    walk(root,root,&root.join("src/native-smoke.ts"),changes)
}

/// The starter README, while it is still one init wrote (for either screen,
/// at the ports it names), when it describes another screen than the schema
/// now gets: the Todos README beside a project's schema, or the reverse.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (042): adapt rewrote the screen but left a README about todos and guest sign-in.
fn readme(dir: &Path, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, modified: &mut Vec<String>) {
    let platform = if dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Web } else if dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() { Platform::Expo } else { return };
    let title = super::title(dir);
    let personas = own.identity.is_none() && !own.personas.is_empty();
    let wanted_body = if todos { Body::Todos } else { Body::Home(None) };
    for path in ["README.md", "README.snapback.md"] {
        let Some(text) = read(dir, path) else { continue };
        let port = number_after(&text, "127.0.0.1:");
        let web_port = number_after(&text, "localhost:");
        let ports = [(port, web_port), (None, web_port), (port, None), (None, None)];
        // The offline paragraph names the schema's synced tables when init
        // wrote it; any of them (or 0.4.22's unconditional claim) still matches.
        let masked = starter::without_offline(&text);
        let explicit_online = platform == Platform::Web && read(dir, "src/client.ts").is_some_and(|client| client.contains("writes: \"online\""));
        let matched = ports.into_iter().find(|&(port, web_port)| {
            [(Body::Todos, false), (Body::Home(None), false), (Body::Home(None), true)].iter()
                .any(|(body, personas)| [false, true].iter().any(|password| [false, true].iter().any(|online| {
                    let readme = starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, body, *personas, &starter::Offline::Unknown, *password);
                    let readme = if *online { starter::online_readme(&readme) } else { readme };
                    masked == starter::without_offline(&super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&readme), port, web_port))
                })))
        });
        let Some((port, web_port)) = matched else { modified.push(path.to_owned()); continue };
        let wanted = super::localize(path, &super::without_design_references(&starter::identity_readme(platform, &title, &wanted_body, personas, &own.offline, own.identity.is_some_and(|(password, guests)| password && !guests))), port, web_port);
        let wanted = if explicit_online { starter::online_readme(&wanted) } else { wanted };
        let wanted = if todos { super::proof::readme(wanted) } else { wanted };
        if wanted == text { continue; }
        let about = if todos { "the todo starter's operations, which your snapback/*.q declares again" } else { "your snapback/*.q and the Home screen, named after this directory" };
        let only_proof = super::proof::without_readme(&wanted) == text;
        let only_offline = !text.starts_with("# Todos") && starter::without_offline(&wanted) == masked;
        // A schema that does not compile right now says nothing about what it syncs.
        if only_offline && !only_proof && own.offline == starter::Offline::Unknown { continue; }
        let why = if only_proof {
            "the unmodified starter README lacks frontend/day-two proof instructions; adapt adds them".to_owned()
        } else if only_offline {
            "the unmodified starter README says what works offline for another schema; adapt rewrites that paragraph from the tables your snapback/*.q syncs".to_owned()
        } else {
            format!("the unmodified starter README describes {}; adapt rewrites it to describe {about}", if text.starts_with("# Todos") { "the todo starter (its todos table and guest sign-in)" } else { "the Home screen for another schema" })
        };
        found.push(Leftover { path, why, rewrite: Some(wanted) });
    }
}

/// An Expo `app.json` that still carries the todo starter's slug, scheme and
/// bundle ids (written by 0.4.21 and earlier): adapt names them after the
/// directory and changes nothing else in the file.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#7.4 — wave 6 (067): adapted Expo apps kept dev.snapback4.todos.
fn app_json(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    let Some(text) = read(dir, "app.json") else { return };
    let Ok(config) = serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&text) else { return };
    let expo = &config["expo"];
    let [(_, slug), (_, scheme), (_, bundle)] = starter::TODO_IDS;
    if expo["slug"] != slug || expo["scheme"] != scheme || expo["ios"]["bundleIdentifier"] != bundle || expo["android"]["package"] != bundle { return; }
    let (new_slug, new_scheme, new_bundle) = starter::app_ids(&super::title(dir));
    let quoted = |value: &str| format!("\"{value}\"");
    let rewrite = text.replace(&quoted(bundle), &quoted(&new_bundle)).replace(&quoted(slug), &quoted(&new_slug)).replace(&quoted(scheme), &quoted(&new_scheme));
    found.push(Leftover { path: "app.json", why: format!("the todo starter's ids (slug {slug}, scheme {scheme}, bundle id {bundle}); adapt names them after this directory ({new_slug}, {new_scheme}, {new_bundle})"), rewrite: Some(rewrite) });
}

// Repair only the exact SVG asset and setting emitted by 0.4.26. Custom
// images/settings remain owned by the app, including other SVG paths.
fn expo_favicon(dir: &Path, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>) {
    if read(dir, "assets/favicon.svg").as_deref() != Some(include_str!("starter/favicon.svg")) { return; }
        _ => Err("declares neither `use identity` nor `use personas`, so nobody can act: add `use identity` for the guest sign-in or `use personas alice, bob` for development, then rerun `snapback4 init --adapt`"),
    }
}

fn screens(dir: &Path, platform: Platform, own: &OwnSchema, todos: bool, found: &mut Vec<Leftover>, advice: &mut Vec<String>) -> bool {
    let (app_path, client_path) = platform.paths();
    let (Some(app), Some(client)) = (read(dir, app_path), read(dir, client_path)) else { return true };
    // Which starter screen is on disk, if both files are still as init wrote
    // them: its sign-in (guest, or a persona picker) and its body.
    let journal_shape = starter::Offline::Tables { synced: vec![], total: 0 };
    let explicit_online = platform == Platform::Web && client.contains("writes: \"online\"");
    let client_shape = normalized(&client);
    let matches_client = |reference: &str, personas: bool| [reference, starter::previous_client(platform, personas)].iter().any(|reference|
        client_shape == normalized(reference) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::web_client((*reference).to_owned(), &journal_shape)) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::online_web_client((*reference).to_owned())) || client_shape == normalized(&starter::expo_client((*reference).to_owned(), &journal_shape)));
    let current = [Body::Todos, Body::Home(first_read(&app))].into_iter().find_map(|body| [0, 1, 2, 3].into_iter().find_map(|version| {
        let screens = |personas: Option<&[String]>, password| match version { 1 => starter::previous_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 2 => starter::wave14_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), 3 => starter::wave20_screens(platform, personas, &body, password), _ => starter::identity_screens(platform, personas, &body, password) };
        let (guest_app, guest_client) = screens(None, false);
        let (persona_app, persona_client) = screens(Some(&[]), false);
        let (password_app, password_client) = screens(None, true);
        if app == guest_app && matches_client(&guest_client, false) { Some((SignIn::Guest, body.clone())) }
        else if app == password_app && matches_client(&password_client, false) { Some((SignIn::Password, body.clone())) }
        else if app == persona_app && matches_client(&persona_client, true) { Some((SignIn::Personas(personas_of(&client)), body.clone())) }
        else { None }
    }));
    let Some((sign_in, body)) = current else { return false };
    // A Home keeps its read while the schema still declares that query
    // argument-free; a Home without one stays as written (it fits any schema).
    let wanted_body = match &body {
        _ if todos => Body::Todos,
        Body::Home(None) => Body::Home(None),
        Body::Home(Some(op)) if own.reads.contains(op) => body.clone(),
        _ => Body::Home(own.reads.first().cloned()),
    };
    // A sign-in no starter screen fits keeps the one on disk, with advice.
    let wanted_sign_in = match wanted(own) {
        Ok(wanted) => wanted,
        Err(why) => {
            advice.push(format!("{app_path} is the starter's {}, but your snapback/*.q {why}", sign_in.label()));
            sign_in.clone()
        }
    };
    let (app_text, mut client_text) = starter::identity_screens(platform, wanted_sign_in.personas(), &wanted_body, wanted_sign_in == SignIn::Password);
    client_text = if explicit_online { starter::online_web_client(client_text) } else if platform == Platform::Expo { starter::expo_client(client_text, &own.offline) } else { starter::web_client(client_text, &own.offline) };
    let client_changed = client_shape != normalized(&client_text);
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && app == app_text && wanted_body == body && !client_changed { return true; }
    let mut reasons = Vec::new();
    let mut client_leftover = None;
    if wanted_sign_in != sign_in {
        let (from, to) = (sign_in.label(), wanted_sign_in.label());
        let because = match &wanted_sign_in { SignIn::Personas(_) => "your snapback/*.q declares `use personas` and no `use identity`", SignIn::Password => "your snapback/*.q declares `use identity password`", SignIn::Guest => "your snapback/*.q admits guests" };
        reasons.push(format!("the unmodified starter screen shows {from}, but {because}; adapt writes {to}"));
        // Keep the backend port init wrote.
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why: format!("the unmodified starter client for {from}; adapt writes {to}"), rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_sign_in == sign_in && client_changed {
        if let Some(port) = port_of(&client) { client_text = client_text.replace("127.0.0.1:4400", &format!("127.0.0.1:{port}")); }
        let why = "the unmodified web client needs the device or principal journal selected by your schema's synced tables".to_owned();
        reasons.push(why.clone());
        client_leftover = Some(Leftover { path: client_path, why, rewrite: Some(client_text.clone()) });
    }
    if wanted_body != body {
        reasons.push(match (&wanted_body, &body) {
            (Body::Home(_), Body::Home(Some(op))) => format!("its Home screen reads api.{op}, which your snapback/*.q no longer declares as a query without required arguments; adapt writes the Home screen again{}", match &wanted_body { Body::Home(Some(next)) => format!(", reading api.{next}"), _ => String::new() }),
            (Body::Home(_), _) => "its Todos screen calls the starter's list/add/toggle/remove, which your snapback/*.q does not declare, so it would not typecheck; adapt writes a Home screen that compiles against any schema (the signed-in viewer, your operations from snapback/generated/api.ts, and your first query without required arguments rendered with matchRead) for you to replace".to_owned(),
            (Body::Todos, _) => "your snapback/*.q declares the todo starter's operations again; adapt writes the Todos screen back".to_owned(),
        });
    }
    let why = reasons.join("; ");
    let why = if why.starts_with("the unmodified") { why } else { format!("the unmodified starter screen: {why}") };
    found.push(Leftover { path: app_path, why, rewrite: Some(app_text) });
    found.extend(client_leftover);
    true
}

/// The `api` path a starter Home's first read calls, if it has one.
fn first_read(app: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let after = app.split_once("function FirstRead()")?.1;
    let op = after.split_once("useQuery(api.")?.1.split_once(',')?.0;
    Some(op.to_owned())
}

fn read(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> Option<String> {
    let path = dir.join(path);
    path.symlink_metadata().ok().filter(|metadata| metadata.is_file())?;
    std::fs::read_to_string(path).ok()
}

/// A starter client without what init or the schema varies: the backend port
/// and the persona list.
fn normalized(client: &str) -> String {
    let lines = client.split_inclusive('\n').map(|line| {
        if line.starts_with(PERSONAS) { format!("{PERSONAS}[];\n") } else { line.to_owned() }
    }).collect::<String>();
    match port_of(&lines) { Some(port) => lines.replace(&format!("127.0.0.1:{port}"), "127.0.0.1:4400"), None => lines }
}

const PERSONAS: &str = "export const personas: readonly string[] = ";

fn personas_of(client: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    client.lines().find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix(PERSONAS))
        .and_then(|list| serde_json::from_str(list.trim_end().trim_end_matches(';')).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
}

fn port_of(client: &str) -> Option<&str> {
    let at = client.find("127.0.0.1:")? + "127.0.0.1:".len();
    let digits = client[at..].find(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(client.len() - at);
    (digits > 0).then(|| &client[at..at + digits])
}

/// One `check` line, or none: the leftovers and the command that fixes them.
pub(crate) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let (found, mut advice) = leftovers(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
    if found.is_empty() && advice.is_empty() { return None; }
    if found.is_empty() { return Some(advice.join("\n")); }
    let mut lines = Vec::new();
    for leftover in &found { lines.push(format!("  {}: {}", leftover.path, leftover.why)); }
    for line in &advice { lines.push(format!("  {line}")); }
    if !found.is_empty() {
        lines.push("  fix: snapback4 init --adapt (removes or rewrites only these files, which still match the starter; your own files are never touched)".into());
    }
    Some(format!("info: starter: {} from `snapback4 init` to review\n{}",
        if found.is_empty() { "a screen".to_owned() } else { format!("{} file{}", found.len(), if found.len() == 1 { "" } else { "s" }) },
        lines.join("\n")))
}

/// The same, for `check --json`: `null` when nothing is left over.
pub(crate) fn report_json(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    let (found, mut advice) = leftovers(dir);
    advice.extend(super::proof_advice::report(dir));
pub(super) const EXPO_ENV: &str = include_str!("starter/expo-env.d.ts");

// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — password identity gets an executable sign-in screen.
pub(super) fn identity_screens(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, password: bool) -> (String, String) {
    let (app, client) = wave20_screens(platform, personas, body, password);
    let app = if platform == Platform::Expo { app.replace("import { useNativeSmokeGuest } from \"./src/native-smoke\";", "import { useNativeSmoke } from \"snapback4-expo/native-smoke\";")
        .replace("useNativeSmokeGuest();", if password { "useNativeSmoke(true);" } else { "useNativeSmoke();" }) } else { app };
    (app, client)
}
pub(super) fn wave20_screens(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, password: bool) -> (String, String) { identity_screens_version(platform, personas, body, password, false) }
// Recognize only complete known templates; edited application source never matches.
pub(super) fn previous_screens(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, password: bool) -> (String, String) { identity_screens_version(platform, personas, body, password, true) }
fn identity_screens_version(platform: Platform, personas: Option<&[String]>, body: &Body, password: bool, previous: bool) -> (String, String) {
    let version = |current, old| if previous { old } else { current };
    if !password { return screens_version(platform, personas, body, previous); }
    let head = match platform { Platform::Web => include_str!("starter/web/password.tsx"), Platform::Expo => version(include_str!("starter/expo/password.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-password.tsx")) };
    let (_, client) = screens_version(platform, None, body, previous);
    let body_source = match (platform, body) {
        (Platform::Web, Body::Todos) => version(include_str!("starter/web/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/web-todos.tsx")).to_owned(),
        (Platform::Expo, Body::Todos) => version(include_str!("starter/expo/todos.tsx"), include_str!("starter/previous/wave9/expo-todos.tsx")).to_owned(),
        (platform, Body::Home(op)) => {
            let (home, first) = match platform { Platform::Web => (include_str!("starter/web/home.tsx"), include_str!("starter/web/first-read.tsx")), Platform::Expo => (include_str!("starter/expo/home.tsx"), include_str!("starter/expo/first-read.tsx")) };
            match op { Some(op) => compose(home, &first.replace("api.OP", &format!("api.{op}"))), None => home.replace("    <FirstRead />\n", "") }
        }
    };
    (compose(&head.replace("<Todos />", if matches!(body, Body::Todos) { "<Todos />" } else { "<Home />" }), &body_source), client)
}

pub(super) fn identity_readme(platform: Platform, title: &str, body: &Body, personas: bool, offline: &Offline, password: bool) -> String {
    let mut text = readme(platform, title, body, personas, offline);
    if platform == Platform::Expo && matches!(offline, Offline::Tables { synced, .. } if synced.is_empty()) {
        text = text.replace("web uses the wasm\ndevice", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
            .replace("web uses the wasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
            .replace("web uses the\nwasm device", "web uses the principal write journal (no synced tables)")
            .replace("or `webDevice()` (web)", "or the principal write journal (web, no synced tables)");
    }
    let text = if password { text.replace(GUEST_SIGN_IN, "Sign up or sign in with email and password (`use identity password`); the SDK\nkeeps your session across restarts. See `snapback4 guide client --section password`.")
        .replace("Choose \"Continue as a guest\", then add", "Sign up or sign in with email and password, then add")
        .replace("Choose \"Continue as a\nguest\", then add", "Sign up or sign in with email and password, then add") } else { text };
    super::proof::readme(text)
}

/// Exact client templates emitted by 0.4.24, before runtime URL/witness additions.
/// Matching complete known bytes preserves the rule that edited files stay owned.
// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000#8 — upgrading an untouched starter still adapts its identity.
pub(super) fn previous_client(platform: Platform, personas: bool) -> &'static str {
    match (platform, personas) {
        (Platform::Web, false) => include_str!("starter/previous/web-client.ts"),
        (Platform::Web, true) => include_str!("starter/previous/web-persona-client.ts"),
        (Platform::Expo, false) => include_str!("starter/previous/expo-client.ts"),
        (Platform::Expo, true) => include_str!("starter/previous/expo-persona-client.ts"),
    }
}

// The smoke's identity choice is generated with the screen, not assumed guest.
pub(super) fn native_smoke(password: bool) -> String {
    native_smoke_version(password, include_str!("native-smoke.ts"))
}

pub(super) fn wave14_native_smoke(password: bool) -> String {
    native_smoke_version(password, include_str!("starter/previous/wave14/native-smoke.ts"))
}

pub(super) fn native_smoke_version(password: bool, guest: &str) -> String {
    if !password { return guest.to_owned(); }
    guest.replace("import { smokeSegment }", "import { smokeAccount, smokeSegment }")
        .replace("void auth.guest();", "const account = smokeAccount();\n    if (account) void auth.signup(account.email, account.password);")
        .replace("sign in as a guest", "sign up with the driver's disposable password account")
}

test("DX5 ReadState presents each list state without losing refusal or partial/stale evidence", () => {
  let received;
  const props = { loading:h("p",null,"Loading"), empty:h("p",null,"Empty"), denied:why=>h("p",null,why.message),
    children:(rows, read)=>{received={rows,read};return h("p",null,rows.map(r=>r.title).join(","));} };
  const render = read=>renderToStaticMarkup(h(ReadState,{...props,read}));
  assert.equal(render({loading:true}),"<p>Loading</p>");
  assert.equal(render(ready([])),"<p>Empty</p>");
  const why={code:"PRIVATE",family:"auth",message:"No access"};
  assert.equal(render({denied:why}),"<p>No access</p>");
  const rows=[{id:"1",title:"Saved"}],read=ready(rows,{existence:"partial",complete:false,fresh:false});
  assert.equal(render({read,items:rows,hasMore:true,loadMore(){}}),"<p>Saved</p>");
  assert.equal(received.rows,rows);assert.equal(received.read,read);
  assert.equal(render(ready([],{existence:"partial",complete:false})),"<p>Empty</p>");
  assert.throws(()=>ReadState({read:{denied:why},children:()=>null}),error=>error instanceof QueryRefusal && error.why===why);
  assert.equal(ReadState({read:{loading:true},children:()=>null}),null);
});

test("DX5 exact published ListScreen snippet renders loading, denied, empty and partial data", async () => {
  const guide=readFileSync(join(dist,"../CLIENT-AND-OPERATIONS.md"),"utf8");
  const snippet=[...guide.matchAll(/```tsx\n([\s\S]*?)\n```/g)].map(m=>m[1]).find(s=>s.includes("function ListScreen"));
  assert.ok(snippet);
  // Transpile the snippet unchanged, then resolve its import to the candidate.
  const compiled=ts.transpileModule(snippet,{compilerOptions:{target:ts.ScriptTarget.ES2022,module:ts.ModuleKind.ESNext,jsx:ts.JsxEmit.ReactJSX}}).outputText;
  const source=compiled.replaceAll('"snapback4/react"',JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(join(dist,"react.js")).href))
    .replaceAll('"react/jsx-runtime"',JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(createRequire(import.meta.url).resolve("react/jsx-runtime")).href));
  const module=await import("data:text/javascript;base64,"+Buffer.from(source).toString("base64"));
  const render=read=>renderToStaticMarkup(h(module.ListScreen,{read}));
  assert.match(render({loading:true}),/Loading/); assert.match(render(ready([])),/No results/);
  assert.match(render({denied:{message:"No access"}}),/role="alert".*No access/);
  assert.match(render(ready([{id:"one",title:"Partial"}],{existence:"partial",complete:false,fresh:false})),/data-complete="false".*Partial/);
});
